Third instrument correction for this family: attributions taken from DMA-less wrong-math legs are void. Frontier ships under a pre-registered contract (shift=0 valid while max_logit + ln(n_terms) < 88.72; silent NaN past ~700-1000x the graded K scale, no runtime guard). Verifier also caught the pinned candidate module untracked in git (fixed pre-verdict; kernel reconstructible bit-identically from history) and that kgate verify-receipt cannot input-regen ANY verify-class receipt on this box. NOT at ceiling: ~0.045 ms still exposed, rank-1 v009 lead is the per-program _validity mask whose (q_head,kv_head) term is grid-invariant yet recomputed on all 64 programs (supported).
- **sparse-attention v006 opus5-process (2026-07-25)**: SUPPORTED with the mechanism REFUTED — the rare split verdict, and the refutation is the valuable half. A 2-wide `fori_loop` body (both QK's issued before either is consumed) advanced the frontier to an independently verified **1.2222 ms / 3.2502x naive / 1.0552x over v004** at **54.5 sigma** (six ordering legs across three processes, sigma 0.0010), all five full-mode gates PASS, parity 0.233x/0.261x of floor on both registered seeds, coverage `causal_blocks` 12/12 + `gqa_isolation` 4/4 with leak 0.0, and the device output **bit-identical to the v004 kernel at eight tile configs including four that exercise the odd-`n_live` `pl.when` tail**. But the stub's pre-registered Predicted signal — "combined matmul time must fall BELOW the sum of the isolated parts" — is falsified: cand2 sits **2.2%** below its own isolated QK+PV sum where v004 already sat **4.9%** below (overlap went DOWN), and the decisive artifact is the verifier's ablation pair showing the identical 2-wide unroll gains **1.110x on a QK-only kernel and 1.070x on a PV-only kernel** — structures containing exactly one matmul, where QK<->PV overlap is definitionally impossible. **TRANSFERABLE (this is the generalizable finding): "the profile says two matmuls are perfectly additive, therefore exposing a second chain will overlap them" is an INVALID inference on v6e when both matmuls are MXU-issue-bound at a half-edge shape** — the additivity is not scheduler-visible slack, it is the MXU serially issuing two dependent half-width matmuls, and no loop restructuring reaches it (two independent routes tried: the explicit carried-score-tile pipeline COST 29%, the unroll won for an unrelated reason). What the unroll actually buys is loop-boundary elimination + denser VLIW bundle packing (static MXU slot rate x1.21/bundle), which is why it pays on ANY matmul stream and why gain does not scale with pair count. Second transferable: the replacement lever is a SHAPE problem, not a scheduling one — **PV costs 1.56x QK at identical FLOPs** (0.679 vs 0.436 ms) because PV's output N = D = 128 is half the v6e 256 MXU edge, worth ~10%. Also measured: **11.6% of wall (0.142 ms) is off-device** — twice this experiment's entire win, never attacked in this family; the 32 MB scoped-VMEM false wall is real and crackable (`kgate fit` locates it exactly, 40.80M fits at 49152 KiB) but **worthless**, `flag_only_ratio` 0.987x = a mild regression on both legs, retiring the family's former rank-1 lead as a MEASURED dead end. Process notes: the author's single-process headline (1.059x) sat 3.6 sigma above the verifier's six-leg mean and its sigma arithmetic divided by per-sample dispersion instead of the ratio's standard error (wrong, safe direction) — both corrected on the page before filing; `get_llo_fit_summary`/`get_llo_static_utilization`/`list_kernel_invocations` ALL still TypeError on this campaign, and the verifier worked around it by shrinking the `--xla_jf_dump_to` footprint 3.1 GB -> 152 MB (pre-generated numpy inputs, no in-process RNG jits) and hand-parsing the static per-bundle utilization file. Family NOT at ceiling: 24.5% of the useful-FLOP envelope, two class levers untried (supported).
- **sparse-attention v008 opus5-process (2026-07-25)**: REFUTED — the off-device axis is CLOSED with the bound CONFIRMED on every term, which is the kind of negative a family needs before it can ever claim a ceiling. The frontier's 0.1403 ms (13.4% of wall) non-device-busy residue was suspected to hide recoverable per-call binding work; cand1 (a rig floor probe -- trivial Pallas kernel, same operands/shapes/grid/BlockSpecs) measured its residue at **0.1401 ms vs the frontier's 0.1403**, and the independent verifier tightened that agreement to **0.000001 ms** and then DECOMPOSED the term: **0.1305 ms is a rig-wide JAX/PJRT per-call cost measured on a ONE-LINE NON-PALLAS `jit(x*c)` at zero device work**, flat across chain depths, with a trivial Pallas call adding nothing to it. **CONFIRMED WALL BUDGET: 1.0487 = 0.1305 rig + <=0.010 binding + 0.9083 device**, summing to the measured wall to 4 decimals, the device term independently corroborated by a chain-depth fit (0.8805-0.8820 ms/invocation) that needs no trace instrumentation. Total kernel-addressable off-device time is **0.95% of wall**, 5.2x short of the 5% bar. The verifier also MEASURED the three envelope-abandoned candidates instead of leaving them inferred -- output donation **0.29%**, operand-count reduction **0.44%**, residue-vs-program-count **FLAT** across 512->128 programs (per-call, not per-program; the stub's prediction was right) -- so three mechanisms now have measured zeros rather than inferred ones. **THE METHODOLOGICAL FINDING (v007's lesson applied one level too shallowly): the floor probe was matched to the frontier on operand count, operand bytes, output bytes AND program count -- precisely the four variables its abandoned candidates would change -- so its residue agreement was GUARANTEED BY CONSTRUCTION and carried no information about them.** The conclusion is right; the author's evidence as filed did not establish it, the verifier's did. GENERALIZED RULE, belongs in BRIEFS beside v007's route-vs-lever rule: *an envelope rule must be scoped to a property no candidate can change, AND its instrument must be free to vary on every axis the abandoned candidates would vary.* Second, subtler catch: the cross-process wall-device convention reports the frontier-vs-probe difference as 0.000001 ms while the device-timeline inter-span gap shows a real **0.0111 ms** body-dependent difference -- the chosen convention cancels the very term the probe existed to detect (now a v011-class lead: it prices a hidden cost of widening the kernel body). cand5 (cross-item prefetch at grid boundaries) NEVER FIRED -- its antecedent was false -- and is carried as an UNTRIED class lever, not a refuted one. **CAMPAIGN-LEVEL CONSTANT: the JAX/PJRT per-call floor on this v6e rig is 0.1305 ms** (paged-attention independently fitted 0.1368-0.1419; same constant, now isolated cleanly on a non-Pallas jit), so any sub-2-ms family's co-measured WALL ratio understates its DEVICE ratio -- 3.788x wall vs 4.23x device here -- and families should state which they quote; max attainable speedup at this op-point is ~30x. Family NOT at ceiling: 28.6% of envelope on wall / 33.0% on device, and the remaining headroom is now provably ALL device-side (refuted).
- **sparse-attention v007 opus5-process (2026-07-25)**: SUPPORTED — **NEW FRONTIER 1.0485 ms / 3.7758x naive / 1.1659x over v006** (+/-0.0013 across 4 fresh processes x 8 orderings, 11.8 sigma vs raw per-iteration dispersion), full-mode receipt all five gates PASS, parity 0.233x/0.261x of floor on both registered seeds, coverage 4/4 + 12/12, and a verifier-authored **972-probe causal-leak battery** over 6 tile configs (4 with odd `n_live`) returning max leak **exactly 0.000e+00** with zero dead rows — the check that mattered because the whole score tile's mask axes were swapped. **THE HEADLINE IS THE PROCESS FAILURE THAT ALMOST HAPPENED.** The author's wrong-math ceiling probe (cand1) measured the registered mechanism at **0.9921x** and an independent route (cand3) at 0.9967x; the stub's own pre-registered rule said `cand1 <=2% => premise false, refute by envelope`, and this page was written as a refute. An **inverted-brief K6 verifier** ("the author is filing a refute; find the win they missed") showed cand1/cand2 had realized the mechanism in the **ONE `dot_general` dimension-numbers form out of four** that captures none of it. **TRANSFERABLE RULE: a wrong-math probe INHERITS THE ROUTE of whatever expresses it** — program.md's "one failed route rules out that route, not the lever" applies to probes exactly as to candidates, and a probe that looks like it prices a lever may only be pricing one expression of it. The instrument that settles it is cheap and neither family had it: a **bare-MXU microbenchmark across the candidate dnums forms at identical FLOPs** (here: 232 / 228 / 296 TF/s for the frontier / cand1's form / the winning form, and 424 TF/s for an N=256 output = **1.79x** the N=128 rate — these bare-MXU figures come from the FIRST K6 dispatch, the route designer, and are corroborative only; the criterion evidence is all from the second, independent dispatch). The mechanism itself is real and device-confirmed (`pltpu.get_tpu_info()` -> `mxu_column_size=256`, so PV's output N = D = 128 genuinely was half-width) but **ROUTE-CONDITIONAL**: flipping N alone buys 0.98x, transposed staging without the flip is a 2.5% REGRESSION, and only "produce the score tile transposed at source so P is naturally (bk,bq) and the PV RHS is canonical" pays (+19.5% in a matched ablation). Confound split, since the same edit moved two things: **~13.7pp matmul package + ~2.6pp** row-sum going cross-lane -> cross-sublane (corroborated structurally: XLU slots **-49.6%**, 1892 -> 954); decomposition closes arithmetically (0.975 x 1.195 = 1.165 = measured). Also: **the module's default entry still resolved to the OLD frontier** (a contract grader got 3.213x with 3.776x one entry away) until the adversarial verifier caught it — check the default arm whenever a mode switch is added. PV contraction-depth folding measured 0.9967x on ONE route whose mechanism was outside the page's registered scope -- recorded as a route result, **the lever is NOT ruled out** (promoting it would be this very page's own anti-pattern). The off-device residue is now shown to be **FIXED not proportional** (0.1415 -> 0.1403 ms while device time fell 15.9%), so its share GREW 11.6% -> 13.4% and it is now rank 1. **Sibling-family action: paged-attention v006 filed this same lever RULED OUT BY MECHANISM on route-level evidence (an xprof-cli regex) and is worth reopening.** Family NOT at ceiling: 28.6% of envelope on wall / 33.0% on device, three class levers untried (supported).
- mla-attention v009 (2026-07-25, o5): REFUTED — the projection matmul chain. Two probes close it: (1) the six projections isolated run 4.1521 ms vs their in-graph bucket 4.127 ms (ratio 1.006), so XLA loses NOTHING at the graph level; (2) out*o_proj, 62.8% of the chain's FLOPs, runs at 82.6% of the 918 TFLOP/s envelope. The four SMALL matmuls do run at 33.9-54.0%, but fusing the two operand-sharing pairs (k_up+v_up on k_lat; q_down+kv_down on x) is neutral-to-negative (0.9983-0.9991x), and with the weights PRE-CONCATENATED outside the timed region the fused matmul gains -0.0001 ms — TRANSFERABLE: XLA already amortizes MXU pipeline fill by scheduling operand-sharing dots back-to-back (65.1% of envelope as a pair vs 51% each alone), so manual N-widening has nothing left to recover. Also: a two-sided pre-registered bar selected by a probe worked well and is worth generalizing.
- mla-attention v008 (2026-07-25, o5): REFUTED — per-item software pipelining. The two-stage interleave fires and captures 78.1% of the available headroom (verifier's same-session control: off 9.3340 -> stage2 9.2311 ms), and a reversed-emission-order ablation is SLOWER (+0.050 ms) so the win is the pipeline, not schedule luck. But cand1 — a wrong-math probe deleting exp while keeping every matmul and the mask — bounds the WHOLE lever at 0.132 ms / 1.018x, ~4x below the 1.05x bar. HEADLINE (shared-knowledge): the kernel-optimization-index hardware envelope's EUP row implies ~1.26 ms of exposed transcendental cost here; measurement says <=0.132 ms — v6e's default VLIW scheduler already hides ~90% of EUP work behind MXU work, so per-unit envelope rows are ISSUE-SLOT CAPACITY, not exposed wall cost. Price a lever with a wrong-math probe BEFORE calibrating its bar (the v007 fix, validated: 1 candidate instead of 6).
- mla-attention v007 (2026-07-25, o5): REFUTED — split-loop/unmasked-prefix. Fires exactly (Mosaic: iota 16->0, mask-cmpi 8->0) but ALL THREE realizations regress: split+const 0.9785x, split+iota 0.9796x, one-emission+resident-mask 0.9847x. Two wrong-math floor probes settle it: deleting the mask entirely from the WINNING structure costs -0.0033 ms, so the 0.23 ms prize never existed — the VLIW scheduler hides mask work behind 0.84 ms of MXU. THREE transferable lessons: (1) a per-element cost anchor does NOT transfer across structures (the bar came from v005's monolithic kernel and was ~70x too optimistic) — price what you are about to remove, in the structure you are removing it from, BEFORE writing the bar; (2) iota beats a resident constant mask on TPU (in-register generation < 2 MB VMEM read); (3) ~0.03 ms per extra matmul emission at this op-point (24->45 instructions cost 0.2 ms).
- mla-attention v005 (2026-07-25, o5): REFUTED — const-shift softmax (C=0). Mechanism fires exactly (Mosaic op census: multi_reduction<maximumf> 1->0, arith.subf 2->0, math.exp 2->1, matmul unchanged); custom-call bucket 4.368->3.907 ms. But 1.0430x vs a 1.05x bar, and 4 launches agree to 0.0072 ms so the 0.071 ms miss is REAL, not noise. Ablation prices the lever: 93%% masked-select+XLU-max-reduce, 5%% the accumulator rescales (which the stub had listed first). Frontier 10.6192 ms / 38.5%% of envelope. Two transferable lessons: a Cauchy-Schwarz bound CANNOT substitute for a measured const-shift margin (loose by sqrt(d) => exp underflows to 0 => NaN denominator), and a fused ones-column denominator is mechanism-blocked when dv already fills a lane tile (dv+1=129 breaks the 128-multiple rule; padding doubles the PV matmul).
- mla-attention v004 (2026-07-24, o5): SUPPORTED — layout-native operands. All five relayout copies deleted by consuming the projections' stored layout (k_nope/v 3-D token-major, q via a free (B,H,D,S) bitcast, output in the consumer's view); 11.0759 ms = 1.1798x over the v003 frontier, 1.5283x over naive, 36.8% of envelope. Lesson: the PRE-REGISTERED q realization (weight-side q_up split -> flat (B,S,H*64) operands) REGRESSED to 0.88x because RoPE's even/odd interleave becomes a tiling-crossing relayout on the flat view — flattening for the kernel's benefit can move more cost into RoPE than it removes from copies.
- **paged-attention v007 opus5-process (2026-07-25)**: the family had quoted its DMA leg as "% of 1.6 TB/s" since v001 without ever measuring what the chip can actually stream; measuring it closed the axis outright — a plain reduction over the same KV arrays achieves **1.3108 TB/s (81.9% of the nominal)**, so the frontier's `dma_only` 0.8433 ms is **97.1% of ACHIEVABLE**, the pre-registered K1 gate (>=95%) fired, and all six planned candidates were abandoned-with-reason **before authoring**; a recorded extension then separated the terms — the linearity control came back 0.8707 rather than ~1.0, and fitting `t = c + bytes/BW` over 128/256/512/1024 MiB (max |resid| **0.0026 ms**) gives a **fixed per-call cost of 0.1336 ms = 14.0% of the wall** (matching the rig's measured trivial-op dispatch floor 0.1195-0.1232 ms) and a **marginal bandwidth of 1.5274 TB/s = 95.5% of nominal**, so the transfer term is **99.1% efficient** and the entire DMA inefficiency is **0.0067 ms (0.7%)**; the resulting **WALL BUDGET 0.9538 = 0.1336 fixed (untouchable) + 0.7097 transfer (at bandwidth) + 0.1105 exposed compute** is the family's first CONFIRMED bound, and it says the only kernel-addressable term left is exposed compute at **~1.13-1.15x**; the verifier reproduced the fit (c 0.1342, BW 1.5552) and went further by **building the abandoned cand1 from scratch** — a Pallas bulk-DMA kernel with **256x coarser descriptors (32768 -> 128)** — measuring 0.8360-0.8451 ms against the frontier's 0.8236-0.8433: **flat**, converting the refute from by-envelope inference into by-measurement fact, and independently endorsing "continue to v008, not close"; three corrections folded — the honest ceiling is **~1.13-1.15x not 1.1310x** (2.6% session drift on both inputs), the fixed cost should be re-derived from a **Pallas** dispatch floor (0.1368-0.1419 ms, which pushes transfer efficiency to ~100%), and "untouchable by any kernel" is scoped to isolated single-call measurement rather than a pipelined deployment; **contradicts the v001-era "~0.21 ms is DMA inefficiency"** framing the family carried for six experiments (refuted).
- **paged-attention v006 opus5-process (2026-07-25)**: the family's rank-1 lead since v001 — "full-width (256-lane) MXU operand staging, <=2.0x" — turned out to be a **TOOL BUG, not a lever**: an independent LLO census of the v005 frontier shows **2048 `vmatpush1` + 2048 `vmatpush2` in the early passes, fusing to 2048 `vmatpush3` (3 = 1|2, the both-halves encoding) at pass 48 `packed-bundles-pre-ra`**, so by xprof-cli's OWN documented heuristic ("matpush1+matpush2 both present -> full 256-lane tile staging") the frontier was **already full-width and always had been**; the "only matpush1 -> 128-lane (half-width)" line came from `llo_dump_tools.py:503` matching `matpush([12])`, which cannot match `matpush3`, then falling through to a hardcoded else-branch string (:612-614) plus a `<=2.0x` STRUCTURAL recommendation (:863) — verifier confirmed by executing the regex directly (NO MATCH), and the **same bug was independently found by the flash-attn-mha v010/v011 verifiers a day earlier**, making it a three-family confirmation that voids every `lane_inference` line in every prior receipt on this box; three structurally distinct realizations were run anyway and all lose — cand1 KV-head lane pairing **0.3304x** because the paired HBM view MATERIALIZES (`%reshape` to `bf16[16384,64,256]` with a dedicated 512 MiB allocation per array = a 1 GiB relayout of the KV cache per call, since `head_dim = 128` is exactly one lane-tile and a 256-lane row must span two interleaved lane-tiles), cand2 K/V sharing one 256-lane staging buffer **0.9071x** with bit-identical output (a 256-lane BUFFER is not a 256-lane PUSH — each contraction slices its own half back out), and cand3 a genuine zero-padded 256-element contraction **0.9997x author / 0.994x [0.990,0.996] verifier** compiling to a **BYTE-IDENTICAL** instruction stream (md5 `6caf0249`, `diff` exit 0, verifier-reproduced from a fresh capture) — a **positive proof of absence** rather than a third failed attempt, which is what makes this *refuted-by-mechanism* rather than under-attempted; the blocking K1 prerequisite (one process per leg, n=150) also corrected v005's attribution — full 0.9538 reproduces to 0.13% but BOTH controls come back 3.1% slower (dma_only 0.8433 = **79.6%** of HBM peak, compute_only 0.5445), so exposed compute is **0.1105 ms = 11.6% of wall**, not 0.1373, and a new `mxu_only` leg (both contractions, softmax deleted) prices the matmul at **0.3695 ms = 67.9% of compute**, i.e. **186 TFLOP/s = 20.3% of v6e bf16 peak DESPITE full-width staging** — which is the v007 lead: **M = 64 query rows against a 256-row MXU edge (pipeline fill)**, the same shape of finding as v004's M=8 result, one step up; frontier unchanged at v005 0.970304 ms (refuted).
- **paged-attention v005 opus5-process (2026-07-25)**: the family's FIRST flag-class experiment cracked the 32 MiB scoped-VMEM default — a false wall on a 128 MB part that had blocked P=256 by 96.0 KiB across three prior experiments — and `--xla_tpu_scoped_vmem_limit_kib=65536` bought an independently verified **1.0986x** (n=400 flagged-vs-flagged, ordering agreement 0.88%, isolated cross-check 1.1160x, all five full-mode gates PASS), advancing the frontier to **0.970304 ms / 7.6991x / 69.2% of the 0.671 ms HBM floor on a wall basis and 83.1% on a device-busy basis**; it is a KERNEL win not a flag win — two independent `flag_only_ratio` measurements STRADDLE 1.000x (1.0024x / 0.9968x), `cand_default` provably cannot compile, and device-time speedup 1.1268x EXCEEDS wall-time 1.0986x which no flag or dispatch artifact can do — and the mechanism is **OVERLAP, not raw speed**: neither leg moved much alone (compute -5.9%, DMA -2.8%) but halving the program count 128->64 and doubling T 2048->4096 leaves half as many pipeline boundaries, raising the hidden fraction of compute 60.1% -> 74.0% and dropping exposed compute 0.2238 -> 0.1373 ms (the same axis v003 won on, reached from the other direction: v003 removed the INTER-sequence boundaries, v005 half the INTRA-sequence ones); parity is stronger than the reference (0.84x/0.90x of the naive's own floor) with 4/4 coverage and an independent randomized-`kv_len` fp64 stress test down to kv_len=1 refuting the nb=1 masking risk; the cost is register pressure (spills+fills 2.15x against a 2.0x width increase, per-bundle 0.51 -> 0.61), which STALES the standing ~1.1-1.26x ceiling estimate for the next lever since that was derived at P=128; depth is now closed on measurement rather than extrapolation (P=128 slots=3, the one cell v003 could not reach, = 0.999x); **the frontier now REQUIRES the flag**, and the verifier explicitly recommends a diagnostic-first re-characterization at P=256 rather than a closing retrospective (supported).
- **paged-attention v004 opus5-process (2026-07-25)**: opened on a K1 RE-characterization rather than the ranked lead — the missing `compute_only` wrong-math leg (complement of v003's `dma_only`) showed **compute is 53% of the kernel** (full 1.0514 / dma_only 0.8246 / compute_only 0.5610 ms), not the ~19% the family had assumed since v001 (that figure measured EXPOSED compute), and that the frontier issues **68.719 GFLOP of matmul for 8.590 GFLOP of useful work = exactly 8.00x = n_kv** redundancy; contracting each query-head group against only its own KV head to remove that redundancy was **REFUTED at 0.6756x** (bar 1.10x, verifier-confirmed on every ledger cell, parity PASS 0.84x floor + 4/4 coverage incl. both GQA group-isolation directions, so a correct-but-slower bound and not a wrong-kernel artifact) — **the 8x redundancy is load-bearing: it keeps M at 64 query heads instead of 8, above the 256-lane MXU pipeline-fill cliff, and the frontier reaches 14.98 useful TFLOP/s against the grouped form's 8.04 DESPITE 8x fewer FLOPs**; a second cost invisible to wall-clock A/B was found by the verifier's static Mosaic instruction census — extracting the per-head view from the (token,kv_head)-interleaved block emits an instruction class the frontier never uses (14,336 `llo.vrot.slane`, 2,048 `llo.slane.shuffle`, 7.1x `vselect`; **+81% total instructions against 4x fewer matmuls**) and scales with block rows, explaining why the penalty grows 1.25x at P=16 to 1.86x at P=128; the matched-granularity control **exonerates strided access** (the clean-reshape form is 5.965x worse, the strided-slice form only 1.248x), the stub's bit-identity prediction was WRONG (3.8-7.6e-6 from fp32 reassociation, inside gate), the spill-coupling speculation came back negative, and P=256 overflows by an identical 96.0 KiB in BOTH formulations — proving the staging buffer not the score tile is that wall, which PROMOTES the scoped-VMEM crack to rank 1; frontier unchanged at v003 1.0642040 ms (refuted).
- **paged-attention v003 opus5-process (2026-07-24)**: the v001 frontier drove its DMA prefetch PER SEQUENCE — priming inside `b == 0` and guarding with `b + 1 < nb` — so at P=128 (grid (64,2)) the last program of every sequence issued nothing and the engine drained at all 64 boundaries, i.e. HALF the programs; driving the DMA off a global block counter `t = s*nb + b` so the prefetch crosses sequence boundaries verified **1.0429x** over the frontier (bootstrap CI95 [1.0417, 1.0449], ZERO resample mass below 1.0, clears 3-sigma of a 1.1-1.3% per-leg CV) but 100% of resamples below the pre-registered 1.06x bar ⇒ **inconclusive with the FRONTIER ADVANCED** per BRIEFS §3, new frontier 1.0642040 ms / 7.1967x / 63.1% of the 0.671 ms HBM floor on an all-gates-PASS full receipt; the DMA-only wrong-math control attributes it cleanly (75.1% -> 82.1% of HBM peak with the attention math deleted, reproduced by the verifier on two methodologies) and the device-busy delta matches the wall delta to 0.5% so it is not a dispatch artifact; **buffer DEPTH is not the binding parameter** (P=64/slots=4 0.998x, slots=6 0.997x — once the schedule is continuous the queue is saturated) and P=128 survived as argmin for a third time, while slots=3 at P=128 is blocked by scoped-VMEM by a now-SIZED 204.0 KiB (P=256 by 64 KiB); the v002-derived structural firing test PASSED (Mosaic body md5 and LLO digest genuinely differ, total bundles 13705->13529 — not compiler-subsumed), but the predicted static-LLO signal moved the WRONG WAY (idle +0.3pp, mem-stall +0.2pp) because the static digest cannot see async DMA engines — second instance in this family that the wrong-math control, not the digest, is the instrument; also surfaced a kgate usability hazard (`verify`'s `--baseline` doubles as the parity-oracle source, so a kernel baseline OOMs the fp32 oracle and reports a misleading parity FAIL), routing next to full-width MXU operand staging (inconclusive).
- **paged-attention v002 opus5-process (2026-07-24)**: the rank-1 lead from v001 — that per-program DMA-descriptor scalar work is a first-order cost — is REFUTED structurally, not statistically: three distinct wait realizations (canonical-descriptor reuse, rolled `fori_loop`, whole-slab aggregate wait) plus a P re-sweep {32,64,128} produced a best of 1.0019x against a 1.06x bar, with 100% of 20,000 bootstrap resamples below the bar and the verifier's independent interleave at 0.99911x (CI95 [0.99764, 1.00095]); the decisive artifact is a digest-vs-digest diff showing cand1 compiles to BYTE-IDENTICAL LLO to the v001 frontier — SALU 32.18%, scalar-only bundles 44.1%, 13705 bundles, spill/fill counts all equal, the only differing line being the `dump_dir` path — because Mosaic's post-GVN passes already deduplicate the repeated page-table SMEM load, making the source-level fix a compiler-subsumed no-op (a silent-no-op class the standard firing audit does NOT catch, since the custom call fires and parity is clean); cand3 then removed 254 of 256 GENUINE wait instructions per program — work GVN cannot elide — for +0.19%, and the DMA-only control held at 0.888 ms / 75-76% of HBM peak, so the 44.1% "SALU bubble" is DMA-wait time charged to whichever unit holds the issue slot; this falsifies `get_llo_fit_summary`'s own <=1.79x ranked recommendation for this lever by ~1.79x and demotes static-occupancy rankings below wrong-math controls as cost attributions; `pl.semaphore_wait` rejects `dma_sem` (gotcha-table candidate), frontier unchanged at v001 1.113354 ms / 60.3% of the HBM floor with 1.66x headroom, routing next to DMA-stream saturation — cross-sequence prefetch at the (64,2) grid boundary, deeper buffering, split K/V semaphores (refuted).
- **gqa-attention v006 opus5-process (2026-07-25)**: causal diagonal subtiling at the v6e MXU edge — **REFUTED at 0.866×, with the bound CONFIRMED from parity-PASSING candidates** and, unusually, with the mechanism firing *perfectly*: emitted `tpu.matmul` 20 → 52 with exactly **20 of 32** subtile bodies surviving (the stub pre-registered "if 32 appear the skip did not fire", and it did not fire), the skip arithmetic brute-force-verified against a causal enumeration before any device time. The attribution control decomposes it exactly and the two effects point opposite ways: **the skip is real and pays 1.0862×** (15% of emitted matmul work deleted) but **retiling alone costs 1.2542× at IDENTICAL arithmetic** (9.3111 → 11.6777 with all 32 subtiles emitted), so the penalty outweighs the gain at every width. Four transferable lessons: (1) **a lever can fire exactly and still lose — 'did the mechanism fire' and 'did the hypothesis hold' are different questions**, and only a no-op control separates them (without cand4 this reads as a failed skip rather than a priced-and-rejected one); (2) **the width sweep is non-monotone with an interior optimum exactly at the MXU edge** (128 → 11.0360, 256 → 10.7511, 512 → 11.0497, none → 9.3111), which CONFIRMS the class page's ordering (≥256, never below the edge) while REFUTING its magnitude — 128 costs 2.7% vs 256, not the cited ~3.5× collapse, because the QK contraction is already `d=128` so the sub-edge penalty is incremental not catastrophic (class-page calibration reported, not edited); (3) **the governing mechanism is thin-in-both-dims**: with `d=128` already below the v6e 256 edge, narrowing the OUTPUT dim 1024 → 256 pays the pipeline-fill bubble 4× as often, and the wide diagonal had been amortizing it — a reusable rule for any causal kernel whose head_dim sits below the MXU edge; (4) **static register pressure is not a timing proxy here** — the stub's second rationale predicted VSTORE:SPILL <30% / VLOAD:FILL <24% from the live score tile shrinking 4 MiB → 1 MiB, measured 37.28% / 24.38%, and the LARGEST spill drop belongs to cand4, the arm that deletes no work. **The search that failed was a real search**: applying this family's own v004 lesson, the verifier got an INVERTED brief and independently built the hybrid the author judged most likely to win (subtile only the `r` values with a genuine skip, leaving `r=6,7` wide); it lowered correctly (40 matmuls, parity PASS) and measured 10.9082 ms — worse than the straightforward arm. Also settled without re-crashing the compiler: the v005 program-size wall is **body-count-bound, not branch-count-bound**. Frontier unchanged at v005's 9.3111 ms; **not at ceiling** (25.7% vs a ~45–50% class yardstick) — `pltpu.emit_pipeline` is untouched and is the rank-1 v007 direction (refuted).
- **gqa-attention v005 opus5-process (2026-07-25)**: layout-native operands — q and the output are consumed in their stored `(B,S,Hq,D)` layout via BlockSpec index maps, deleting 2 of the 4 `swapaxes` relayouts (k/v keep theirs) — independently verified **9.3111 ms / 1.1641x over the 10.8380 ms frontier / 3.7589x naive** (25.7% of the useful-FLOP envelope), `kgate verify --mode full` all five gates PASS, reproduction MATCHES (1.0003), coverage 20/20 with every unaffected region bit-identical, plus a verifier-authored **28-position causal-leak probe** (0 leak at every bq/bk/group boundary +-1). **This is the rare case where the stub was right about the cause AND the size** (predicted 1.08-1.14x central against a 1.172x hard ceiling; measured 1.1641x): non-kernel on-device time collapsed 1.595 -> 0.0445 ms/iter (-97.2%), the `data formatting` bucket left the top-ops table entirely, kernel share of wall rose 83.0 -> 96.95% (beating its own ~95% prediction), and MXU occupancy held at 75.76% vs v003's 75.89%, proving the body really was fixed while only the boundary moved. Four transferable lessons: (1) **a 'blocked-by-lowering' verdict can be one arithmetic insight away from a win** — 4 of 11 candidates died at COMPILE time (3 on a compiler program-size SIGSEGV at 80 emitted bodies, 1 on the 32 MB scoped-VMEM default = false-wall STEP-0) until the static `lax.switch` was **BUCKETED on `i//(bk/bq)`**: `(n_full,n_kv)` depends on the q-block index only through the bk-aligned super-block, so v003's switch-on-`i` was right only because it had `bq==bk`, and once the flat realization forced `bq<bk` it emitted 28 duplicate branches out of 32. Bucketing restored v003's exact program size (4 branches / 10 bodies) and took the mechanism from uncompilable to +16.4% in one edit — had the run stopped at cand2's crash this lever would have been filed a false negative on the family's largest measured open cost; (2) **the K4 interpret gate is blind to the failures that actually dominate BlockSpec/grid work** — a second independent instance class after v004's (program-size + VMEM now, lowering constraints then); interpret never lowers, never allocates, has no program-size limit, and `kgate fit` (compile-only, already in the toolbox) is the missing gate leg; (3) **sub-tile access is the governing constraint of `(B,S,H,D)` layouts, and it cuts both ways** — the win came from R3's `(bq,hp,d)->(bq*hp,d)` reshape being a genuinely FREE bitcast (verified: `tpu.reshape` with IDENTICAL in/out layout tags, all 36 sites gone by `post-apply-vector-layout`, zero `vector.transpose`), while the same tiling kills the K/V half by mechanism — `Hkv=8` means the only legal head-block is the full axis, so selecting a head is always a 1-of-8 **sublane slice** that Mosaic relayouts inside all 2048 programs (cand1c: correct kernel, 109.81 ms, 10.1x SLOWER — a parity-PASSING measurement, hence a real bound and not a broken implementation); (4) **a warm-tier refutation does not transfer when its mechanism is absent** — codex v007 refuted heads-per-program on this exact problem, but hp is measured NEUTRAL here (hp=16 ties within 0.16%) because the multiplied-K/V-reload mechanism behind that regression cannot exist under a revisit-eliding index map; the stub pre-committed to exactly this falsifiable reason and it held. Attribution was clean twice over — the author's in-file control AND an independently rebuilt verifier control both reproduce v003 (0.25% / 0.19%), the verifier's control is **bit-identical in output** to the winner, and co-measured they price the boundary at 1.1673x / 1.1687x, so the stub's own named confound is measured at 1.0025x = nothing. Also filed: `kgate verify` writes `inputs_sha256`/`outputs_sha256`/`thresholds` as **null**, so `kgate verify-receipt` reports `STATUS: MISMATCH` on EVERY `verify` receipt in the campaign while self-hash passes and verdict is PASS — a LINT-void hazard for all four prior verdicts here, with the real input hashes present in `verify_full.json`'s legs. **Not at ceiling** (25.7% vs a ~45-50% class yardstick, LLO verdict TUNE), but the boundary lever IS exhausted (0.48% of wall), routing next to body-level register pressure: spill/fill 37.7/28.3% co-occurring with MXU 75.76% ⇒ causal diagonal subtiling >=256 / `pltpu.emit_pipeline` (supported).
- **gqa-attention v004 opus5-process (2026-07-25)**: deepen the operand pipeline via `BlockSpec(pipeline_mode=pl.Buffered(...))` to close v003's 18.0% MXU-idle mem-stall — **REFUTED, and the mechanism turned out UNAVAILABLE rather than merely weak**: `buffer_count>2` fails to lower (`LoweringException: Only single (1) and double (2) buffering are supported`, `lowering.py:1228` — double buffering is already the default, so there is **no depth headroom at all** on the `pallas_call` path however much VMEM is free), `use_lookahead=True` fails to lower (`NotImplementedError: Lookahead is not supported for XLA pipeline emitter lowering`, `lowering.py:1223`), the third `pl.Buffered` field `revisit` (untried by the author, probed by the verifier) lowers but is inert across 4 legal pairings in an 8 µs band — its own docstring says input blocks ignore it — and the one legal depth deviation `buffer_count=1` **regresses 1.30×**. The declared structural alternative `dimension_semantics=(parallel,parallel,arbitrary)` measured **exactly 1.0000×**, and the pre-registered subsumption control explained it: the `parallel` vs `arbitrary` Mosaic dumps differ in **ONE LINE across ALL 19 compiler stages** (out to the 142,567-line `lower-to-llo`), with the LLO digest byte-identical to v003 — VMEM still 1.07 MiB, MXU still 75.89%, and the targeted mem-stall still exactly 18.0%. Three transferable lessons: (1) **the LLO digest's ranked lever recommendations are hypotheses about ROUTES, and routes are exactly what the compiler may not expose** — its bucket *sizes* have been accurate every time, but its recommended levers are now 0-for-3 in this campaign (paged v002 ≤1.79%→1.0019×, gqa v003 rank-1 wrong causal story, gqa v004 route absent); put a cheap *availability* check (does the knob even lower?) at the top of any tool-suggested plan, before the tile sweep and before the control; (2) **the K4 interpret early-kill gate is structurally blind to Mosaic lowering constraints** — it PASSED both configs that are hard TPU compile errors, because `pipeline_mode` is BlockSpec metadata interpret never lowers (gate should compile cand1's skeleton on TPU, not just check custom_call presence); (3) **a refute is only as strong as the search that failed** — an inverted verifier brief ("this is a refute, find the win I missed") produced three arms the author never ran, including the strongest overlooked candidate. Bound confirmed is deliberately NARROW: this *route* to the mem-stall is closed, not the bucket — `pltpu.emit_pipeline` (the manual API that does honour `pl.Buffered`) is a different structure and remains untried. Frontier unchanged at v003's 10.8380 ms / 22.1% of envelope (refuted).
- **gqa-attention v003 opus5-process (2026-07-25)**: split-loop / unmasked-prefix — the causal `fori_loop` splits into a mask-free hot loop over fully-visible KV blocks plus a masked diagonal loop (`n_full = (i*bq)//bk`, brute-force-verified over the whole sweep grid), deleting `iota`/`cmpi`/`select` and the loop-invariant 4 MiB `q_pos` tile from 6 of 10 traced block pairs — independently verified **10.838 ms / 1.1279x over the 12.2249 ms frontier / 3.2421x naive** (22.1% of the useful-FLOP envelope), all full-mode gates PASS, firing CONFIRMED at Mosaic **per-block** granularity, plus a verifier-authored **27-position adversarial causality probe** (block boundaries ±1, two perturbation magnitudes) returning bit-identical unaffected regions. **The headline lesson is that the DECLARED MECHANISM UNDERDELIVERED and the DECLARED STRUCTURAL ALTERNATIVE won**: mask deletion alone measured 1.0290x — *below* the 1.06 bar — while making the loop bounds compile-time constants (one `lax.switch` branch per q-block index, which removes the `fori_loop` carry and makes every K/V slice static) cleared it at 1.1267x. A 2x2 factorial, author-run and independently reproduced by the verifier, prices staticness 1.0512x, mask-deletion 1.0477x, both 1.1471x — **super-additive by 1.042x** (with the mask present the body is big enough to spill regardless; only once it shrinks does static scheduling pay). Four lessons: (1) **the LLO digest's own rank-1 recommendation was misleading** — it said "restructure the live-register set", but spills barely moved (1.82 -> 1.67, and got *worse* at 1.94 on the declared-mechanism arm) while the real win was **loop-carry elimination unlocking static MXU pipelining** (MXU 51.12 -> 75.89%, mem-stall 39.6 -> 18.0%, `verdict_class` flipping VPU-serialized -> compute-bound); (2) `fori_loop(unroll=N>1)` **requires static bounds** and dies on grid-dependent ones, while **`unroll=1` succeeds silently as the no-op default** — a `{default,1}` sweep reports "non-lever" having never engaged the knob (new gotcha-row candidate); (3) **never inherit a control** — the in-file all-masked arm did NOT reproduce v002 (12.4468 vs 12.2249, 3.6 sigma across 3 replicates) because `q_pos` had to move inside the loop, exposing a separable ~1.8% hoist micro-lever that would otherwise have inflated every attribution on the page; (4) tile sensitivity re-derived exactly as BRIEFS warns — bk=512's penalty collapsed +13.0% -> +1.5% under the static structure while bk=2048's grew +9.1% -> +17.0%, optimum still interior at 1024. The `swapaxes` relayout is now **14.7% of wall** (second consecutive experiment where its fixed cost grew as a share) (supported).
- **gqa-attention v002 opus5-process (2026-07-24)**: const-shift softmax (C=0, overflow margin pre-registered at 12.47x before authoring and independently re-derived) deleted the running-max bookkeeping from v001's streaming loop — the cross-lane XLU max, the `s − m` score-tile subtract, both carry rescales and the `m` carry itself — for an independently verified **12.2249 ms / 1.2090x over the 14.7799 ms frontier / 2.877x naive** (19.6% of the useful-FLOP envelope), all full-mode gates PASS; the firing audit was run at **Mosaic instruction level against the running-max arm of the SAME source file** (an int `shift_mode` switch, never a bool — the sibling family's voided-control trap), and every predicted op-census delta matched exactly (multi_reduction`<maximumf>` 2→0, `arith.subf`→0, `math.exp` 2→1, carry `mulf`→0) while every invariant held byte-identically (matmul/iota/cmpi/select counts, grid bounds, BlockSpec transforms `diff`-empty), so the delta is attributable to the mechanism and nothing else; that same in-file control co-measured **14.7934 ms, reproducing the banked frontier to 0.09%**, which is the cleanest available answer to "is this a cross-session artifact". Three lessons: (1) the tile ranking is **tile-driven, not bookkeeping-cost-driven** — the stub predicted the optimum would move toward larger bk once per-block bookkeeping got cheaper and it did NOT (all four neighbours of 1024x1024 regress again, though bq=2048 did improve 16.94→13.10 ms, so the live-set shrink is real but second-order); (2) **the bound was dented, not removed** — MXU 41.32→51.12%, mem-stall 50.5→39.6%, but VALU is still 53.63% and spills only fell 2.23→1.82/bundle, leaving `verdict_class=STRUCTURAL` with "restructure the live-register set" still rank-1; (3) **EUP occupancy ROSE** (25.83→28.76%) despite strictly fewer `exp` call-sites, because total bundles shrank 19.2% faster than the transcendental work did — a caution against reading a unit's occupancy share as its absolute cost, and against expecting more from the softmax-deletion direction. Also newly quantified: the `swapaxes` relayout tax is ~1.61 ms/iter = **13.2% of wall, on-device**, vs only 1.8% host dispatch (supported).
