- **mla-attention v010 opus5-process (2026-07-25)**: heads/batches-per-program — the LAST untried lever on the routed class page — fires structurally (shipped Mosaic `iteration_bounds` 4,128,1,1 -> 1,128,1,1 = 512 -> 128 programs, `tpu.matmul` 24 -> 96 per program at an unchanged 12288 total, per-program MXU-idle stall 9.2% -> 5.1%) and delivers a REAL margin the verifier proved is not noise (interleaved cold-launch A/B: bp=4 arm 9.2050-9.2173 vs bp=1 arm 9.3158-9.3307, completely disjoint, separation ~7-8x either arm's spread) — but at 1.0096x verified / 1.0120x matched it is HALF the pre-registered 1.02x bar, so inconclusive exactly as the stub pre-registered for a real-but-sub-2% result. THREE transferable findings: (1) batch-in-block BEATS heads-in-block at identical program count (9.2329 vs 9.2638) — BRIEFS' batch-vs-head sign-flip confirmed, and the declared structural CONTROL beat all four arms of the mechanism it was controlling for, the 3rd time in this family the realization mattered more than the lever; (2) operand-traffic levers are dead at this op-point — the arm that quarters k_rope traffic is 0.03 ms SLOWER than the arm that leaves it alone, and the verifier showed the shipped batch fold does not reduce k_rope traffic AT ALL (the BlockSpec keeps the batch axis, so the block grows exactly as the program count shrinks; the stub's ~0.04 ms 'identifiable component' does not exist and the whole 0.11 ms is per-program fixed cost); (3) `custom_call_count` is 10 for BOTH naive and candidate here because XLA lowers the baseline's own softmax to a `tpu_custom_call` — a count-based firing audit false-negatives on any XLA-`online-softmax` family, use the `pallas_call` op_name + the absent forbidden shape. Frontier unchanged at 9.3475 ms; best verified parity-passing advances to 9.2585 ms (3rd occurrence of the LINT-vs-BRIEFS Current-best collision). The deepest fold (bp=4 x hp=2, 64 programs) is blocked at 44.00M vs the 32MB scoped-VMEM DEFAULT on a 128MB part = textbook false wall, and with half-width (128-lane) MXU staging measured on BOTH structures, cracking that flag is the precondition for the lane-pairing lever that is the family's one remaining multiple.
- **mla-attention v011 opus5-process (2026-07-25)**: **NEW FRONTIER 8.5376 ms / 1.9805x vs naive / 1.0949x over the v006 Current best**, full-mode receipt OVERALL PASS on all 5 gates, 60.2% of the v6e bf16 peak. The kernel had been issuing TWO QK contractions per emission (128-deep q_nope*k_nope + 64-deep q_rope*k_rope); this concatenates them along the contraction axis IN VMEM, pads to 256, and issues ONE deep MXU push. Firing CONFIRMED on the shipped Mosaic payload (tpu.matmul 24 -> 16 per program, LLO staging 'only matpush1 -> 128-lane half-width' -> 'matpush1+matpush2 -> full 256-lane tile', bundles -31%, spills -44%) and the entry-module HLO is structurally byte-identical to v010's (md5 match, zero new concatenate, custom-call operand_layout_constraints unchanged) so the concat provably never left VMEM and v003's graph-level savings are not given back. **HEADLINE, transferable: the v6e MXU bills the PASS, not the contraction depth** - 192->256 deep is FREE (-0.0021 ms despite 33% more nominal FLOPs), 128->256 deep costs 0.41%, but splitting the same 192 of depth across two pushes costs 9.3%. That is a twice-measured (v002 weakly, v011 decisively) CORRECTION to kernels/classes/attention.md, which advises 'composite head dims pad to the tile edge - split per-component contractions on the direct route'; it should be shape-conditioned on whether the composite depth fits one tile edge. Why 9 experiments missed it: v002 measured the composite as better, but v003 moved to the split because the split bought a -1.79 ms formatting saving that dominated - the trade LOOKED forced and the two levers were never tested apart. Procedure: the wrong-math ceiling probe selected the branch for the 4th time (v007/v008/v009/v011) and for the first time pointed TOWARD the work, pricing the prize at 0.8239 ms before a line was written; the candidate captures 95.8%, leaving 0.41% - contraction-depth levers are now exhausted. The ablation (k-concat, split dots kept) lands SLOWER than base, proving operand assembly is a cost and the whole gain is the single pass. The verifier also caught an author single-shot inversion (cand3 appeared faster than the wrong-math floor, physically impossible) and restored the ordering by interleaving - two arms 0.02 ms apart are not resolvable from one launch each.
- **mla-attention v012 opus5-process (2026-07-25)**: **NEW FRONTIER 7.5284 ms / 2.2536x vs naive / 1.1379x over v011**, full-mode receipt OVERALL PASS, ~54% of the v6e bf16 roofline, ~30 sigma with disjoint cold-launch arms. Moves the decoupled RoPE rotation OUT of the XLA graph and INTO the Pallas kernel (un-rotated q_rope + two grid-invariant 128 KB cos/sin operands). Firing CONFIRMED: 11 rotation ops on vector<32x2048xbf16> in the shipped Mosaic payload vs ZERO in v011's, tpu.matmul unchanged at 16, device slice bucket 1.1665 -> 0.0013 ms/iter. **HEADLINE: elementwise work is FREE on an MXU-bound structure but HBM traffic is not** - the rotation cost +0.0093 ms (+0.5%) while deleting 1.165 ms of slice and 0.376 ms of data formatting; this also CLOSES the EUP lead NEGATIVELY (static EUP 41.51% -> 41.47%, unmoved). **NEW GOTCHA (for the shared table): a stride-2 slice of a VMEM value is UNLOWERABLE in Pallas/Mosaic** - `'vector.extract_strided_slice' op expected strides to be confined to [1, 2)`; the way around it is to move the interleave into the WEIGHTS, where it is free (pre-permute q_up columns to [evens; odds]; k_rope carries the identical permutation and the contraction sums over the rope axis so the dot is unchanged - proved bit-identical by construction, and 5 single-mutation controls fail at 116-159x floor, so the pairing is load-bearing). **TWO SELF-REPORTED FAILURES worth more than the win**: (1) the pre-registered convolution-fusion confound control FAILED at +0.528 ms because the page re-derives the permuted weight as an in-graph GATHER every iteration - 0.770 ms = 10.3% of runtime, and the verifier already measured the bit-identical pre-permuted endpoint at 6.7336 ms, so v013's rank-1 lead ships with its answer attached; (2) **cand1 was NOT a strict ceiling** - the correct-math pre-permuted kernel (6.7336) beats the wrong-math probe (6.7746), refining the family's four-times-validated procedure: a wrong-math probe bounds the LEVER IT DELETES, not the route you end up taking. Also: the in-kernel rotation is bf16 where the graph's was f32, so parity moved 0.026584 -> 0.029684 (+11.7%, still 0.67x floor = 3x margin) - a real precision reduction the stub did not predict.
- **mla-attention v013 opus5-process (2026-07-25)**: **NEW FRONTIER 6.8387 ms / 2.4789x vs naive / 1.1008x over v012**, receipt OVERALL PASS, coverage 12/12, output BIT-IDENTICAL to v012. Expressing a compile-time reindex of the q_up weight as STATIC STRIDED SLICES instead of a dynamic gather on an s32[192] index vector: %gather.2 1->0, gather_fusion 2->0, the pad/clamp/AssumeGatherIndicesInBound chain gone, 226.5 MB of permutation-chain materialization -> 151.0 MB fused straight into the q-up projection; convolution fusion 4.6375 -> 4.0187 ms/iter with the Pallas custom-call unchanged (pure graph lever). Transferable: XLA will NOT constant-fold a permutation of a runtime PARAMETER in either form - what changes is whether the permuted buffer must be materialized separately - and the `slice` bucket is a misleading proxy (static weight slices ADD 0.11 ms there while REMOVING 0.62 ms from convolution fusion; read bucket deltas together). **THE PROCESS STORY IS THE RESULT: this page was first filed as REFUTED on measurements taken through a driver bug.** run_plan.sh interpolated an unassigned $qkf, so every candidate was written with qk_form='' - matching no legal form - and silently ran the PRE-v011 split kernel ~0.8 ms slower, swamping the lever's real 0.69 ms gain and flipping its sign. The K6 verifier caught it; I did not, even though the tell (a candidate output hash diverging from the frontier's on a change I had argued was bit-neutral) was in my own artifacts. Both apparently-failed predictions were CORRECT, and a 'fusibility' theory drafted to explain the artifact is explicitly NOT propagated - a tidy explanation for a surprising number is the moment to re-check the measurement, not to write it up. TWO FREE DEFENSES ADOPTED, transferable to every family: (1) validate every config string against its legal value set before the run; (2) assert each candidate's output hash against the frontier's whenever the declared change is bit-neutral. Contamination bounded mechanically, not by argument: a sweep of ALL 70 committed CAND lines family-wide (64 pre-v013 + 6 on v013) found exactly 2 degenerate config strings, both v013, both superseded - v011/v012 stand.
- **mla-attention v014 opus5-process (2026-07-25)**: **NEW FRONTIER 6.3925 ms / 2.6505x vs naive / 1.0713x over v013**, receipt OVERALL PASS, 12/12 coverage, output BIT-IDENTICAL, ~37 sigma. Splits the q up-projection into TWO dots at the weight so the bf16[4,2048,128,192] combined q (402.65 MB) is never materialized. **Firing confirmed with QUANTITATIVE BYTE CLOSURE**: device `loop fusion` 0.5624 -> 0.0073 ms/iter carrying 805.1 MB/iter of eliminated traffic against an arithmetic 805.3 MB = 1.45 TB/s = 91% of v6e HBM peak, exactly a streaming write+read of that array; ENTRY materialized 1833.96 -> 1347.42 MB; the Pallas custom-call AND its serialized Mosaic payload are byte-identical to v013 (sha d36cb3ad...), so this is a pure graph lever and the kernel binary has now been unchanged across THREE consecutive frontier shifts. **GENERAL FORM, transferable: when a producer's output is immediately sliced into disjoint pieces by its only consumers, split the producer instead** - the split is exactly re-associable so it is free correctness-wise. Reverses v004 (weight split refuted at 0.88x on the pre-v011 structure) - the family's 4th cost-anchor-does-not-transfer instance - **but the verifier REFUTED my stated reason**: toggling qk_form=split reproduces the same 0.4541 ms absolute gain on the pre-v011 in-kernel form, so the win does NOT require v011's composite contraction; it is pure graph-side HBM traffic, and the real enabler is the D-major-via-bitcast path that removes v004's relayout. **THREE SELF-REPORTED DEFECTS worth more than the number**: (1) cand3's prediction missed (85% of the gain, not 50%) AND the width explanation I gave was refuted - XLA sank the consumed slice into the still-combined dot, so the ablation fired the same mechanism and cannot isolate what it was declared to isolate; (2) cand4 was a DEGENERATE control - its optimized HLO is byte-identical to the baseline's except a metadata line because XLA canonicalizes concat(slice,slice) back, so it could not fail and licenses no inference (a control that cannot fail is worse than none: it manufactures false confidence); (3) one of the stub's three claimed enablers is measured-false. CEILING: the projection chain is independently at 81.7% of the v6e bf16 envelope; structural floor 3.34 (projections at peak) + 1.83 (kernel) = 5.17 ms vs 6.35 measured, so ~1.19 ms remains, of which only ~0.26 ms is graph-side. Every remaining named lead is INSIDE the 1.83 ms kernel.
- **mla-attention v015 opus5-process (2026-07-25)**: **refuted — and the most cost-effective page in the family: a lever the LLO digest had rated rank-1 STRUCTURAL for four experiments, CLOSED BY DIAGNOSTIC WITHOUT AUTHORING A SINGLE CANDIDATE.** cand1 classified all 2758 post-RA spill stores by producing opcode: **61.8% are `vpop.eup` = p_sub (the exp result), 92% are p_sub plus its row-sum reduction tree, and acc_ref contributes ZERO spills** (512 st + 512 ld of ordinary vmem traffic, decomposing exactly as init + 8 RMW + finalize). p_sub is (256,k_w) fp32 = up to 512 vregs against a 64-vreg file, compiler peak pressure 354 = 5.5x the file, with TWO consumers (bf16 pack into the PV matmul, and the lane reduction). **THE BOUND, from the compiler's own counterfactual**: the LLO dump emits alternate packings, and `pre-ra` (ideal, containing zero spill/fill instructions) is 5548 bundles vs 5864 post-RA, so ALL register-allocation cost is 307-316 bundles = **0.090-0.093 ms** at 512 programs / 1.74 GHz against a 0.1253 ms bar — **perfect execution of the digest's recommendation reaches 74% of the bar**. Bundles~cycles calibrated two independent ways (absolute: the static schedule accounts for 93.0% of measured kernel time; differential: v011's split-vs-concat256 bundle delta predicts 0.753 ms vs a measured 0.7208). Zero bundles in the program exist solely to carry spill/fill traffic and 96.8% of spill-carrying bundles co-issue an MXU op, so the traffic is hidden. Corroborated by an already-measured parity-passing arm: the only structure that shrinks p_sub is row_grouped=False, which v006 cand1 measured at 10.763 vs 9.341 ms — the minimum-register-pressure realization is 15% SLOWER. **TRANSFERABLE: a static analyzer's rank-1 recommendation is a ranking among the things it can see, not a claim about what is worth doing** — the digest could not see that p_sub's width is exactly what the causal-skip structure buys, nor that the spill traffic is fully co-issued. 6th application of the family's probe-first procedure, 2nd time it returned 'do not build'. **Relocates the remaining in-kernel headroom**: MXU issue slack is 1186 bundles = 0.349 ms (3.8x the register-pressure block), MXU-idle 714 bundles = 0.210 ms, prologue 396 bundles = 0.117 ms, plus a provably-DEAD acc init+RMW worth ~0.03 ms (subtile forces nq==nkv==1 and row_grouped emits one k-span per subtile, so every acc slice is written exactly once against _init's zeros).
- **mla-attention v016 opus5-process (2026-07-25)**: **refuted at its own pre-registered gate on the first candidate** — frontier unchanged at 6.3925 ms. The wrong-math ceiling probe (delete `exp`, keep every matmul, the mask and both accumulator updates) buys **0.046 ms** against a 0.125 ms bar; the stub had predicted >=0.25 ms and pre-registered <0.125 ms as an immediate refute. **THE DIRECTIONAL FINDING: v008 measured this same quantity at 0.132 ms on the pre-v011 split-contraction kernel, so the exp-shaped cost has SHRUNK 2.9x** — the composite contraction (v011), in-kernel RoPE (v012) and producer split (v014) had already absorbed most of the serial matmul->pop->scale->exp->pack->matmul dependence that pipelining targets. Consequently **v015's 0.349 ms of MXU issue slack splits into 0.046 ms exp-shaped (13%) and ~0.30 ms of operand staging/packing that no exp reordering can reach** — which correctly re-scopes what had been the family's rank-1 lead. cand2-cand5 all abandoned with the reason produced by the plan's own gating item: each is a realization of the mechanism cand1 priced, and v008 established the mechanism captures ~78% of available headroom when it fires, so the best possible arm is 0.036 ms — below the 0.037 ms 3-sigma band. **TRANSFERABLE, and a prediction I got backwards: 'a cost anchor does not transfer across structures' says nothing about DIRECTION.** I used it to argue the prize would be larger; it was 2.9x smaller. The rule licenses re-measuring, not re-predicting. 7th application of probe-first, 3rd time it returned 'do not build' — and the three 'do not build' verdicts (v015, v016) between them retired two leads that had stood for four experiments, at a cost of one diagnostic and one probe.
- **mla-attention v018 opus5-process (2026-07-25)**: **supported — NEW FRONTIER 5.9034 ms AT `--xla_tpu_scoped_vmem_limit_kib=65536`**, co-measured 2.7748x vs naive (bar 2.7035) = 1.0469x over v014, receipt OVERALL PASS, 12/12 coverage, output BIT-IDENTICAL to v014. **First `flag`-class arm since v001** — which measured the NAIVE, not a kernel — closing a lead named in **15 consecutive pages**. **The real result is cand2's compile-only ceiling probe**: `bp=4 x hp=2` fails at the default with `requested 44.00M vs limit 32.00M` (independently reproducing v010 cand6) and COMPILES at 65536/98304/131072, so the BRIEFS false wall lifts and both v017's shared-latent arm and v010's fold are unblocked. **FRAMING, adjudicated by the verifier rather than the author**: the raw-p50 reading (6.3925 -> 5.9034 = 1.0817x) is DISALLOWED by program.md's Gates — a co-measured ratio is authoritative, never an absolute ms from another run under a different compiler configuration — so the number of record is the co-measured 2.7748x and the p50 is valid only written WITH THE FLAG NAMED; recording a bare 5.90 would make the next v-level compare flagged against unflagged and manufacture a phantom regression (the 37k failure mode one level up). **83% OF THE GAIN IS NOT KERNEL WORK, measured on the device**: the flag moves the Pallas kernel 1.8285 -> 1.8287 ms (+0.01%) and the XLA projection chain 4.089 -> 3.689 ms/iter, and the naive collects 0.573 ms from the identical knob. The margin is also CONTINGENT ON THE FLAG VALUE: at 98304 — v001's optimum and what a process-global deployment would plausibly pick — it clears the bar by 0.0004x and sits BELOW it against the verifier's own unflagged re-measure. **TRANSFERABLE: the kernel's optimum (65536) is NOT the naive's (98304)** — v001 measured the naive and this family carried its answer forward for 17 experiments without noticing the operand of the claim. **Deployability, measured not asserted**: `XLA_FLAGS` FATALLY ABORTS for this flag (`Unknown flag in XLA_FLAGS`), `LIBTPU_INIT_ARGS` is process-global, and `compile(compiler_options={...})` is per-executable — the last is the deployable route and is UNTIMED. The verifier additionally REFUTED the author's reading that the fold is unbankable: held with the flag fixed on both legs, interleaved in-process over 5 launches and BOTH leg assignments, the fold is 1.0113x = 0.0668 ms = 1.8x the 3-sigma band and ~11 sigma of observed sd, corroborated on-device at -0.0691 ms; the author's 0.048 ms came from differencing against a single leg with std 0.4878. The verifier also hit and disclosed a harness bug in its own first attempt (two wrapper modules both doing `import kernel` share one module object, so `CAND` was shared and it measured the same executable twice, returning 1.0002).
- **v009 constant-hoist out of the S² score tile** — refuted. Mechanism fires exactly as pre-registered (`vmul.f32` −97.1%, every other op count bit-identical) and is worth 1.0045× isolated at the shipped tile / 1.0345× at 1024, never clearing its own 1.05× bar: the multiplies were already hidden under MXU issue (occupancy 53%→82%). Refuted with the mechanism PROVEN PRESENT, not inferred absent. Same page cracked a FALSE VMEM wall (`kgate fit`: bq=bk=4096 fits) and reached bq=bk=S at 3.2921 ms / 1.4314×, ~99.7% of it the tile — routed to v010 as a distinct mechanism. Verifier found an unclaimed MXU half-width staging regression (≤2.0×).
- **v010 per-program overhead / heads-per-program** — refuted. Mechanism fires on every structural axis (grid programs 256→128, per-program matmuls and bundles ×2.00, VMEM ×1.99, grid shape confirmed in HLO) and delivers **1.0005×** against a *quantitative* prediction of 1.078×. The pre-registered discriminating control (batch-packing, which halves programs identically) independently agrees at 1.0001×. LLO shows the packed program is exactly two copies of the unpacked one — there is no per-program fixed cost to amortize. By elimination, v009 tile win = intra-program scheduling depth → v011. Withdrew v009 rank-2 MXU half-width lead as a digest classifier bug.
- **v011 live accumulator set / per-row-subtile writeback** — **supported, new frontier 2.8829 ms / 5.3565× / 41.5%**. Replacing the diagonal nest's list-and-concatenate with a direct per-row `o_ref` store cuts the live set from 2.0 MiB to one 128 KiB slice: verifier-confirmed 1.1383×, neutrality control 0.9999×, mechanism isolated 1.1380× (closes to 0.04%). Spills 0.88→0.58/bundle, mem-stall 12.3→2.1%, `vmatmul`/`vpow2` bit-identical. **Method finding**: the obvious implementation was 40% slower and only its neutrality control revealed why (per-row `fori_loop`s broke the single straight-line 136-subtile nest) — measure the restructure before the mechanism. Attribution: the lever is general/additive (1.1483× at 1024 too), so v009's tile win remains unexplained after three attempts.
- **v012 sub=128 on the rowwrite structure** — refuted (0.1336×, a 7.5× collapse) but diagnostically decisive. Every predicted sub-effect fired (matmuls 4352→4224 = −2.94%, closing the emitted-work arithmetic exactly; spills 0.58→0.13/bundle; VMEM unchanged) while bundles went 18666→148509 and MXU occupancy 93.26→11.38% with 53.3% idle. **v004's never-below-the-256-MXU-edge rule is confirmed on two structurally different kernels** and v009 cand10's live-set reading is retracted. **Work axis CLOSED**: 53.125% emitted vs a 50.01% causal-useful floor, last 2.9% unreachable.
- **v013 live score tile** — refuted (0.7838×, wrong direction). Matching the frontier's 256 KiB score-tile footprint at a **fixed grid** cost 27% instead of recovering the 1.37× tile win; 2048 agrees (0.6728×). **Five** mechanisms are now independent of that win. **The direction is the finding**: identical sub=256 granularity helps at 4096 (straight-line, whole kernel) and hurts at 1024/2048 (inside a dynamic `fori`) → straight-line scheduling depth is the last hypothesis standing. Also fixed a ten-experiment deliverable defect: `workload` (kgate's auto-resolve target) still bound v003's ~6.76 ms kernel; it now names the frontier, verified at 5.3493×.
- **v014 straight-line scheduling depth** — **supported (mechanism), PARTIAL (explanation), frontier unmoved**. Lifting the q-block walk out of the grid (a `fori_loop` whose trip count is `pl.program_id`-dependent) into a trace-time Python loop recovers **1.3592×** of the family's unexplained 1.3714× tile win **at a fixed bq=bk=1024 tile**, landing within 1.010× of the frontier. Firing confirmed *in the compiled binary* — the verifier decoded the Mosaic MLIR and found `dimension_semantics<arbitrary>` 1 → 0. Attribution is mechanistic, not correlational: MXU static occupancy **63.31 → 73.48 → 92.23 → 95.01%** and mem-stall **18.7 → 8.4 → 0.7 → 1.1%** across grid-walk / grid-folded-prefix-dynamic / prefix-unrolled / bq=512, with the OFF-switch control sitting exactly between ON and OFF on every axis. The mechanism's value **grows with prefix depth**: 1.22× @1024, 1.85× @512, 3.57× @256. **Two standing family rules fall**: the tile optimum is *structure-relative* (4096 on the dynamic path, **512** on the straight-line path), and "every axis trading unit size for unit count loses" is false on this structure — v004's monotone regression and v012's collapse were properties of the dynamic-`fori` structure, not of the op. **The pre-registered METHOD-RULE control FAILED** (the restructure at bq=bk=4096 is a no-op by construction, yet came in at 0.8359×, verifier-confirmed), so the tile win decomposes into **two** separable multiplicative effects — grid-fold 1.1148× × prefix-unroll 1.2201× = 1.3602× vs 1.3592× measured — and ~16% of the restructure remains un-named. Frontier **NOT advanced** despite a verifier-measured **2.8318 ms** (1.0180×, real at 43× over 3σ of the run-to-run ratio std) — below BRIEFS' unconditional sub-2% floor; the verifier was asked and adjudicated "may NOT advance". **Stop-rule condition 2 is NOT discharged** — ~62% of the tile win explained in log terms; the 16% residual is the rank-1 v015 lead.
- **v015 grid arity / loop wrapper / redundant slice** — **refuted, and the sharpest refutation the family has produced.** Three pre-registered structural suspects for v014's unexplained 16.4%, all firing, **all exactly neutral**: a dummy size-1 innermost `arbitrary` grid dimension **1.0002×** at bq=4096 *and* 1.0002× at bq=512 (bar was 1.10×), the 1-trip `fori_loop` wrapper **0.9998×**, the redundant whole-array q slice **1.0001×**. The OFF-switch control — shipped FIRST this time, per the rule v014 earned — reproduced the gap at 0.8358× vs v014's 0.8359×, so the module is clean and the effect is stable across three independent sessions. **Refuted on its own predicted signal**, not just its bar: it predicted mem-stall would be *higher* in the 2-D-grid kernel; mem-stall is *lower* (1.2% vs 2.1%), as are spills (0.48 vs 0.58/bundle), VLOAD and VSTORE, while VMEM (8.07 MiB) and matmul count (4352) are byte-identical. **Positive result: the residual is PURE SCHEDULE LENGTH** — bundles 18666 → 22527 (+20.7%) against +19.6% time, tracking to within 1% at identical arithmetic and memory footprint; Mosaic issues the same 4352 matmuls across ~3861 more VLIW bundles on the straight-line-at-top-level path. Memory, pipelining and register pressure are excluded **by measurement**. **Methodological rule earned**: at fixed matmul count, MXU static occupancy and bundle count are the *same measurement* (93.26/77.28 = 1.2068 vs 22527/18666 = 1.2069 — four decimals) — citing both as corroborating evidence is double-counting, which several prior pages in this family did. Stop-rule condition 2 still NOT discharged; next lever is a bundle-composition diff via `get_llo_bundles`.
- **v016 constant-mask materialization** — **refuted, and with v015 it closes the 16.4% question by elimination.** The registered route (traced mask offsets via `pl.program_id` on a size-1 grid dim) was **constant-folded away** — a size-1 dim's `program_id` is provably 0, so it compiled to a **byte-identical** program (22527 bundles, MXU 77.28%, spills 5073+5640) and measured **1.0000×**. Per program.md that rules out the route, not the lever — **so cand4 refuted the lever independently**: converting both digests to *absolute occupied slots* gives MXU 17409 vs 17408 (**+1 in 17409**), VALU −417, EUP −256, **VLOAD −15 (flat)**, VSTORE −68, SALU −64. The slow kernel does the same MXU work and **less of every other kind** in **3861 more bundles**; flat VLOAD kills the mask-load story outright. **The extra bundles are EMPTY.** Residual named: **Mosaic scheduler packing density** (~93% matmul density wrapped vs ~77% flat) — not a kernel lever but an empirical property of the substrate, recorded as such per v014's pre-commitment rather than inventing a seventh mechanism. Six candidate causes now excluded by measurement across v015+v016 (memory, register pressure, grid arity, loop wrapper, redundant slice, mask constants). **Process finding**: the HLO firing audit structurally cannot detect a *mechanism-level* no-op when the kernel fires either way — `custom_call_count` was 1 for a byte-identical program; an LLO-digest diff against the control would catch it.
- **v017 subtile axis at the 512 tile** — **refuted, and it closes the family's last untried lever.** 512 became the best tile only at v014 and only `sub=256` had ever been measured there. `sub=512` (diagonal nest 3 `(256,256)` ops → 1 `(512,512)`, +5.9% emitted work for 2/3 fewer scheduling boundaries) **loses at 0.9361×** — within a point of the 0.944× predicted by the extra work *alone*, so packing returned essentially nothing. **Key narrowing: the v016 packing effect operates BETWEEN scheduling structures; it is not a currency that buys extra arithmetic WITHIN one.** `sub=128` collapses to **0.5389×**, confirming *never subtile below the 256-lane MXU edge* on a **third** structurally different kernel (v004 0.402× grid-walk, v012 0.1336× at 4096, v017 0.5389× straight-line at 512) — the one family rule v014's structure-relativity finding did NOT overturn, principled because the MXU edge is a hardware constant, not a scheduling artifact. **`sub=256` is an interior optimum at the best tile, confirmed from both sides.** Frontier unchanged at 2.8829 ms; v014's verified 2.8318 ms still withheld by the 2% floor. **Stop-condition 1 satisfied for the first time in the family's history**; conditions 2–4 remain as separate artifacts.
- **flash-attn-mha RETROSPECTIVE #2 (closing, 2026-07-25)** — family **AT CEILING** after 18 experiments: naive 15.837 ms → frontier **2.8829 ms = 5.3565×**, **41.5% of nominal / 75.0% of the measured scale-matched ceiling**, 93.26% static MXU occupancy, 2.1% mem-stall, 94% device-busy. All four stop conditions satisfied with artifacts: (1) 11/11 class levers tried-or-ruled-out, auditor set-diff clean, both latest pages state no leads remain; (2) bound confirmed — work axis closed on a parity-PASSING candidate (53.125% vs a 50.01% floor, last 2.9% costs 7.5×), issue efficiency above the class page's 70% test, **and retro #1's rank-1 shape-matched roofline probe retired as UNANSWERABLE BY CONSTRUCTION** (it is bandwidth-bound at that geometry — the kernel IS the measurement); (3) frontier unmoved since v011 with retro #1's recommendations attempted (rank-1 at v014, rank-2 at v018); (4) two independent full-mode receipts all-gates-PASS (5.3477× master, 5.3260× adversarial verifier) plus an original overflow-margin stress test (NaN cliff at max|s| ≈ 92 vs a 6.8 op-point — real margin, fail-loud). **A verified faster kernel is deliberately withheld**: v014's 2.8318 ms at 1.0180×, real at 43× over 3σ but under the unconditional 2% materiality floor, verifier-adjudicated "may not advance". Transferable lessons: tile optima are **structure-relative** (optimum moved 4096→512 when the q-walk left the grid, inverting v004); straight-line scheduling depth is a first-class Mosaic lever (1.22×/1.85×/3.57× by depth); never subtile below the MXU edge (three structures); at fixed matmul count MXU occupancy IS bundle count; a lever refuted on one base is not refuted on another (conclusion transfers, magnitude does not).
- **gqa-attention v008 opus5-process (2026-07-25)**: **supported — new frontier 7.0402 ms / 4.9650x naive / 34.0% of the useful-FLOP envelope, 1.3310x co-measured over v005, the family's largest single-experiment gain.** The PV contraction's OUTPUT dim was `d=128`, exactly HALF the device-confirmed v6e `mxu_column_size=256`; producing the score tile TRANSPOSED AT SOURCE (`s^T = dot(k,q)`, `out^T = dot(v,p^T)`) makes it `n_rows=1024`. Verifier full-mode all gates PASS, reproduction MATCHES 1.0031, firing CONFIRMED **in the compiled Mosaic body** (`named_scopes` = transpose+concatenate+dot_general, ROOT is the custom-call so the transpose is in-kernel, zero `4096,4096`), parity **tighter than the naive's own** (0.466 vs 2.84-2.9 bf16 ULP), and an independent 18-position causal-leak probe **at the real op-point** returning exactly 0 (the mask axes swap in this structure — the pre-registered "most likely way this is silently wrong"). **THE MANDATORY WRONG-MATH CONTROL DECOMPOSES IT FOR THE HYPOTHESIS**: removing the final in-VMEM transpose gives 6.9623 vs the correct 7.0187, so the transpose costs **1.0081x against a 1.3266x mechanism — 1:41**, the exact question v006 answered the other way (1.2542x retile vs 1.0862x skip). **THREE SEPARABLE SUB-RESULTS, each with its own arm.** (1) *The denominator fold pays 5.2% in the DECLARED realization and is NEGATIVE in the other realization of the same invariant*: a `(bk,8)` ones block on `v` (so `l` falls out of the PV matmul) gives 7.7319 -> 7.3469, while an `(8,bk)` ones **matmul** gives 8.5359 — worse than the plain VPU row-sum it replaces. What pays is that `l` comes out of a matmul ALREADY BEING ISSUED; an M=8-degenerate second matmul pays a fixed per-matmul cost that swamps a 1M-element reduction. BRIEFS calls this choice op-point-sign-flipping; here it sign-flips across REALIZATIONS at one op-point. (2) *The tile optimum MOVED 1024 -> 512*, the first time this knob's argmin has moved in five re-derivations, interior from both sides (256 -> 45.9876 a CLIFF at parity-PASS/cc=1, 512 -> 7.0187, 1024 -> 7.3469, 2048 -> 8.7891). (3) *The in-kernel transpose beats XLA's by 25x* (0.056 vs 1.403 ms) — a resident (128,1024) VMEM tile vs a 537 MB HBM tensor; v005's boundary lesson reproducing on the OUTPUT side. **WHAT IT DOES NOT ESTABLISH, and the process lesson**: the bare-MXU probe never converged (1.0036 -> 1.0330 -> 1.0909 over three revisions, all <=9% of peak against a kernel at 25.7% of its envelope), so there is no transferable isolated half-width estimate — and the probe's pre-registered `<1.15x => refute` branch was **DECLINED with the bar HELD at its strictest 1.05x**, which is the decision that made a 1.33x lever reachable. Filed as a process gap, not a virtue: **a pre-registered rule keyed to a TIMING ratio inherits whatever regime its instrument lands in, so it needs an instrument-validity precondition**; an LLO-digest-keyed rule (v007's) is regime-free and did not need one. **NOT AT CEILING** (34.0% vs a ~45-50% class yardstick, LLO class STRUCTURAL): rank-1 lead is the live-register-set/accumulator-chain split, now backed by a FRESH v008-specific 1.34 spills/bundle (2.68x the flag) — carried with an explicit counter-prior, since `mla-attention` v015 CLOSED that lever by diagnostic while `flash-attn-mha` v011 SUPPORTED it for +13.8%, so v009 must classify WHICH values spill (by producing opcode) before authoring. Operand-feed width ruled OUT as a second helping (`matpush1`+`matpush2` both present). Also retires this family's previously-nominated at-ceiling argument: `d=128`-below-the-MXU-edge was **reorientable**, not structural.
- **gqa-attention v009 opus5-process (2026-07-25, verified 2026-07-27)**: **refuted, bound CONFIRMED from parity-PASSING candidates — the LLO fit's own rank-1 `[STRUCTURAL]` "restructure the live-register set / split the accumulator chain" is DEAD at this op-point, and the cheapest candidate is what killed it.** The BLOCKING cand0 diagnostic priced the lever **before a line of kernel code**: the `jf_dump` emits **four** counterfactual schedules of the same program (`pre-ra` 33906 / `no-ra-deps` 34969 / `no-spills-fills` 34991 / `post-ra` SHIPPED 36461), decomposing RA cost into an **attackable** part (spill/fill instructions, 1470 bundles = **4.03%** = 0.272 ms) and an **unattackable** one (register-*assignment* constraints, 2.98%, present with ZERO spill instructions — reduced ILP from reusing a 64-vreg file, which no source change removes). Against the 0.335 ms bar the attackable part is **81%**: perfect execution could not have cleared it. Census: peak pressure **837 vregs = 13.1× the register file**, top value the `exp` result (`p^T`, 2 MiB, live range 6815, **exactly one user**) — 8× the whole file, so it can never be register-resident; the **accumulator is not the pressure source**; and of 35193 bundles 25376 (72.1%) carry spill/fill traffic while **zero exist solely to carry it** (100% co-issue other work, 86.8% co-issue an MXU op vs an 86.5% all-bundle rate — spilling displaces **no** MXU issue). The five arms measured exactly that: cand3 accumulator split **1.0046×** (best; under the 1.05× bar AND the 2% materiality floor), cand1 key-axis subtile at the 256 edge 0.8342×, cand2 below the edge 0.5877×, cand5 row-axis chunk 0.8608×. **The mandatory control decomposes cand1 completely**: cand4 runs the subtile path at `sub=bk=512` (identical arithmetic, matmul count, live tile) and is **exactly neutral, 0.9978×** — the restructure is FREE here and the whole 20% is **contraction depth**, which retires v006's 1.2542× retiling penalty as a property of the UNTRANSPOSED structure rather than of subtiling. cand5 is the decisive alternative: it shrinks the live set by a route that never touches the MXU edge and **still costs 14%** — the live tile's *width* is what this structure buys. cand2 collapses below the edge on a **fourth** structurally distinct kernel (flash-attn-mha v004 0.402× / v012 0.1336× / v017 0.5389×, now 0.5877×), holding the never-below-the-edge **ordering** at magnitude 1.70× not the class page's ~3.5×, because the transposed structure keeps output N at 1024 so the matmul goes thin in ONE dim not two. **The verifier (after a 2-day account-quota block and two supervisor-SIGTERM'd attempts) built SEVEN more arms and closed two axes the author never reached**: `accsplit>2` is provably non-improving (4 neutral/negative, **8 measurably WORSE at 0.963×**, CI95 [0.9620, 0.9639]) and the **hp/bq re-tile axis is CLOSED** (`hp` hard-capped at {8,16} by the kernel's own asserts; the only untested legal point hp16/bq64 is neutral; off-design `bq` 64 → 0.82×, 256 → 0.39×). Best of **twelve** configurations = 1.0033–1.0046× measured three ways (kgate receipt 1.0037×, 60-round paired bootstrap 1.0038× CI95 [1.0024, 1.0048], device-busy 1.0035×) — a **real** effect whose CI excludes 1.0, an order of magnitude short of the bar. The verifier also **re-captured the entire LLO dump from scratch** (fresh process, different seed): bundle counts within 0.09%, MXU opcode census **bit-for-bit identical**, and the decisive "zero bundles exist solely for spill/fill" reproduces **exactly**; three independent derivations of attackable-vs-bar agree within 1.5 pp (81.1% / 82.5%). **Transferable, and this is the second family to reach it**: `mla-attention` v015 closed this same recommendation by diagnostic at 74% of bar, `gqa-attention` v009 at 81% — while `flash-attn-mha` v011 SUPPORTED it for +13.8%. **The discriminator is whether the spilled value is the score tile itself.** Process gaps filed: a rule keyed to a compiler counterfactual must NAME THE EXACT PAIR — `post-ra − pre-ra` over-counts, the attackable bound is `post-ra − no-spills-fills`; and `--xla_jf_dump_to`/`--xla_jf_dump_llo_text` are **fatal in `XLA_FLAGS`** and must go via `LIBTPU_INIT_ARGS` (BRIEFS §6 says the opposite for `--xla_dump_to`, which is what mis-routed it). Frontier **UNCHANGED at v008 7.0402 ms**; **NOT at ceiling** (34.0% of the useful-FLOP envelope vs a ~45–50% class yardstick) — rank-1 v010 lead is **QK contraction depth `d=128` at half the v6e 256-lane MXU edge**, carrying the verifier's caution that `get_llo_fit_summary`'s half-width regex has produced FALSE verdicts on already-full-width kernels in 3 other families (refuted).
- **mla-attention v017 shared-operand expansion-in-kernel on the compressed latent (2026-07-27)** — **refuted, and it closes the cell retrospective #1 found mis-recorded.** The family had carried "expansion-in-kernel — tried, v003" since v004; v003 applied it to the 64-wide RoPE component, not the 512-wide latent the class page names. Now measured: the mechanism **fires exactly** (verifier-derived — the custom-call takes `bf16[4,2048,512]` + one `bf16[512,16384]` weight; graph convolutions emitting `bf16[4,2048,16384]` go 2 → 0 → 1 for off/'both'/'v'), is parity-clean (0.6666x of the naive's own floor, 12/12 coverage, **output bit-identical to the v018 frontier**), moves device time in the predicted direction — and **its entire ceiling is ~0.8-1% against a 2% bar** (verifier full-mode 2.7985x vs a 2.8303x bar, `verdict: FAIL` on the bar gate alone, all four other gates PASS). **The registered full mechanism REGRESSES at 0.9862x** while each half alone gains — v-only 5.8595, k-only 5.8827, both 5.9858 vs a 5.9034 frontier — a **+0.147 ms non-additive interaction**. Its dominant term is measured to be **graph-side, not kernel-side**: pulling up-projections out of XLA's own convolution-fusion batch has sharply diminishing marginal returns (-0.180 ms for the first, -0.053 ms for the second), which **refutes this page's own stated VMEM/spill explanation** (static VSTORE:SPILL falls 21.91% -> 19.99% under 'both'; the kernel is just +8.65% bundles of ordinary per-expansion overhead). **Three transferable process findings**: (1) a `vs frontier` column computed as *hardcoded historical p50 / fresh p50* is the BRIEFS §1 cross-launch trap and it nearly became this page's headline — the verifier's co-measured ratio is what caught it; (2) the first firing gate counted a shape (`bf16[4,2048,16384]`) that is also the kernel's OUTPUT, so it could not have failed in either world — replaced before any arm ran, third occurrence of the degenerate-instrument pattern in this family; (3) a real 0.048 ms margin at ~20 SEM (paired same-process co-measurement, 450 samples) is still **3x below** what a 2% bar demands — statistical reality and materiality are different questions. Frontier UNCHANGED at v018's 5.9034 ms; **best verified parity-passing time is now 5.8430 ms** and withheld by the sub-2% rule — the family's **third** occurrence of that LINT-vs-BRIEFS collision.
- **ragged-paged-attention v002 opus5-process (2026-07-27)**: **supported — new frontier 4.2042 ms / 4.0569x naive / 14.2% of v6e bf16 peak, 1.1500x co-measured over v001, and the win is ONE LINE.** The rank-1 v001 lead was the per-head KV de-interleave, priced at 0.9671 ms = 21.3% of the wall: `kv_pages` stores 16 combined KV heads on the TILED second-to-last axis, so `blk[:, 2g, :]` is a sub-tile SUBLANE slice done 16x per staged block. One bulk `jnp.transpose(blk,(1,0,2))` after landing makes every `Kg`/`Vg` a free LEADING-axis slice and **recovers 0.6306 ms = 65.2% of the leg** (stub pre-registered >=40%), margin to the 1.08x bar = **11.1 sigma**, output **BIT-IDENTICAL** to the frontier (same `outputs_sha256` both seeds). **THE FIRING AUDIT IS `PARTIAL`, NOT `CONFIRMED`, AND THAT IS THE TRANSFERABLE LESSON.** Three of four predicted signals landed — the targeted cross-sublane class fell `vrot.slane` **-41.05%** / `vsel` **-70.18%** / **-52.86% combined**, wall cleared the bar, output bit-identical — but the fourth, "XLU slot occupancy DOWN in `get_llo_fit_summary`", is **literally false**: it went **UP**, 22.77% -> 26.52%. Not a mechanism failure: total bundles fell **-16.8%**, FASTER than the retained XLU-class instructions, so a bundle-**fraction** metric inverted while the **raw count** it was meant to proxy halved. **Predict RAW INSTRUCTION COUNTS, never occupancy percentages, when the intervention also shrinks the bundle stream** — an occupancy prediction is a ratio whose denominator your own change is moving. **THREE CLEAN NEGATIVES LOCALIZE THE 21.3% LEG BY ELIMINATION**, which is most of this page's durable value: it is **NOT cross-tile selection** (cand3 tile-aligned half-split `blk[:,0:8,:]`/`blk[:,8:16,:]` = 0.9989x — Mosaic already lowers the sub-tile slice tile-locally), **NOT scheduling** (cand4 hoisting group g+1's extraction above group g's contractions = 1.0001x — the Mosaic scheduler already reorders across the unrolled group loop, **RULING THE CLASS PAGE's per-item-software-pipelining LEVER OUT at this op-point**, opposite to its verified -24% paged-class precedent), and **NOT a strided-slice lowering problem** (cand2/cand2b dead on both paths). **TWO NEW MOSAIC GOTCHAS, both transferable to any bf16 kernel**: `'vector.extract_strided_slice' op expected strides to be confined to [1, 2)` — *Mosaic's vector dialect has no strided slice at all*; and `tpu.strided_load` -> `not implemented: Strided load with non 32-bit data` — *strided ref loads are 32-bit-only, hence unavailable in ANY bf16-graded kernel*. Together they close the "strided sublane slice" route on BOTH lowering paths. P sweep (cand5 P=32 -> 0.7939x) confirms P=64 stays the interior optimum under the cheaper extraction. **The verifier cleared two adversarial checks either of which could have voided the result**: (1) the frontier-bar module `frontier_v001.py` delegates its non-bf16 branch to `baseline.workload` (because the frozen v001 Pallas kernel genuinely cannot compile at fp32 — its staging scratch doubles past the 32 MiB scoped default), which could have made parity vacuous; line-by-line diff of the **bf16** path vs v001 `dcbb544fa` shows **zero functional differences**, and candidate/frontier emit the **same bits**, which no loosened gate can fake; (2) the two `verify-receipt` **MISMATCH** reports are a **kgate schema gap, NOT fabrication** — `receipt.build()` never populates `inputs_sha256` for `cmd == "verify"`, so **every** verify-class receipt in the wiki fails input-regen by construction (confirmed across `mla-attention`/`retnet-retention`/`gqa-attn`/`flash-attn-mha`); **third independent family to reach this finding**, and it collides directly with LINT's fabricated-receipt class. Two further tooling gaps filed: `xprof-cli get_llo_fit_summary`'s **CLI wrapper** throws `TypeError: slice indices must be integers` on `--top_stalls` (Fire not coercing; the underlying `_fit_data()` is fine), and its `op_counts` still misses `matpush3` — benign here only because `lane_inference` reached the right full-256-lane verdict via the matpush1+matpush2 co-presence heuristic, after the raw LLO was hand-grepped. **NOT AT CEILING, and the profile sharpens it rather than softening it**: at 14.2% of peak the frontier is 7.02x the compute floor and 4.68x the bandwidth envelope, and `get_llo_fit_summary` returns **`STRUCTURAL — VPU-serialized` for BOTH the frontier and cand1** (1.44 spills+fills/bundle, 37.9% of bundles still `mem_stall_mxu_idle`) — a **register-pressure lead that is NOT on the routed class page's lever list**, now rank-1 and gating the multi-buffering lever behind it. ~0.336 ms (34.8%) of the extraction leg remains; class levers 2 (transposed score tile), 3 (const-shift softmax), 4 (split-loop/unmasked-prefix) and 6 (fold the qT/oT transposes) entirely untried. Rank-1 v003 = transposed score tile => cross-sublane softmax reductions (sibling `sparse-attention` v007 verified +19.5%).
- **mla-attention v022 same-mix schedule packing density (2026-07-27)** — **refuted AT THE MECHANISM LEVEL, and it closes the in-kernel side of the family.** v021 attributed the long-unexplained ~0.30 ms to the **7 318-bundle / 16.6% gap between the schedule and its binding MXU-port floor**; this page asked whether any control moves that gap at a FIXED instruction mix, and pre-registered that a negative answer counts only if backed by **three structurally different controls**. None moved it by the pre-registered **2 917 bundles** — a bar deliberately stated **clock-free**, so the rule-out does not inherit the campaign's contested bundles→time constant. **(1) Source-level emission order** (a new `emit_order` knob on the shipped loop; the CAND surface's own `pipeline='reverse'` is UNUSABLE because it is wired to the pre-v011 split contraction — the family's 4th degenerate-instrument catch, found before any arm ran): `rev` +156, `alt` −174 bundles, 17–19× short. **And the registered same-mix gate FAILED for both — which is the finding**: arithmetic totals hold to ≤0.07% while FILL/SPILL move +3 899/+1 283, because reordering changes live-range overlap and therefore register-allocation traffic. **A source-level reorder is structurally unable to satisfy a slot-total same-mix gate on this substrate.** The mechanism argument survives it: the MXU-port floor is a function of per-subtile instruction COUNTS, which are order-invariant by construction. **(2) Pallas `dimension_semantics`** `arbitrary`→`parallel`: **exactly zero** change (44 128 → 44 128, all ten unit totals identical) — the degenerate-instrument shape this family has now hit five times, and **the page could not close it but the verifier built the instrument that could**: an MLIR-body diff showing `#tpu.dimension_semantics<arbitrary>` present in the frontier's compiled Mosaic body and ABSENT in cand2's (different length, different SHA). The flag demonstrably reached the compiler; the null is real and explained (a grid axis of size 1 admits no cross-step pipelining decision), making this the STRONGEST of the three controls rather than the weakest. **(3) Ten scheduler flags** across two independent `strings`-derived searches (6 author + 4 verifier of ~45 examined, from 1 064 in libtpu), all inert in the improving direction — against **TWO negative controls that fire hard**: `xla_jf_naive_bundle_packer=true` → **294 683 bundles = 6.68× worse** (≈ the program's total op count, i.e. exactly one op per bundle) and the verifier's independently-found `xla_jf_vliw_scheduler=false` → 84 837 (1.92×), plus a crash stack (`xla_jf_slot_tracker_hoist_limit`) landing inside `jellyfish::BundlePacker`. **The VLIW packer is LIVE and already compressing 6.68×; the IMPROVING direction is what is exhausted.** A bogus-flag control (hard `ERROR: Unknown command line flag`) rules out silent flag-ignore for every flag tested. **TRANSFERABLE, for any family reading `-70-final_hlo-static-per-bundle-utilization.txt`: (a) a slot-total "same-mix" gate is unsatisfiable by source reordering — register allocation is part of the mix; (b) `strings libtpu.so | grep '^xla_(tpu|jf)_'` is a working flag-discovery vector (1 064 flags here), and `xla_jf_naive_bundle_packer` is a ready-made negative control that proves whether a packing lever is live at all; (c) an exactly-null bundle count is only evidence if you can show the knob reached the compiler — the decoded `custom_call_config.body` MLIR diff is how.** **THE FAMILY IS NOT AT CEILING**: asked directly, the verifier **REFUSED to sign off on a whole-family close** — the graph-side projection chain is **62.0% of frontier device time against the kernel's 29.6%**, and has had ~4 narrow fusion-pairing arms across 3 pages versus ~20 pages aimed at the kernel; v017/v020's "the chain is one scheduling unit" is a real finding about per-dot fusion pairing, not an exhaustive search of the largest block on the device. That lead is propagated as family rank 1 and becomes v023.
- **mla-attention v021 per-bundle census of the MXU issue slack (2026-07-27)** — **refuted, and it finally ATTRIBUTES the ~0.30 ms block that three experiments left unexplained.** The composition fork was real and could have gone the other way (`flash-attn-mha` v016's empty-slot reading was likelier on v017's evidence and would have closed the in-kernel side by inheritance); the shipped payload answered **staging, 55.6% of non-MXU-issuing bundles**, which OBLIGED a build. Both build arms then regress — `subtile=512` 2.7573x / 0.9940x, `subtile=1024` 2.6113x / 0.9407x vs the 5.9034 ms frontier — **parity PASS on both**, so this refute confirms a bound rather than a broken implementation. **THE STRUCTURAL RESULT, transferable to any MXU-bound attention kernel: the MXU issue port is the binding unit (83.4% occupancy, floor 36 811 of 44 129 bundles) and its composition is fixed by arithmetic rather than authorship — `vmatmul` is exactly 12.5% of the port and `vpop.mxu` exactly 4x `vmatmul` at EVERY subtile size measured.** Only one eighth of the binding port is the multiply; half is draining the accumulator, and the drain is captive to matmul count. So coarsening emissions to amortize operand staging *buys staging by paying accumulator drain*, at a measured exchange rate of **2 592 staging slots for 10 877 vpop+matmul slots** — the regression is structural, not a tuning miss. **The ~0.30 ms is now attributed**: not exp-shaped (v016: 0.046 ms), not register allocation (v015: 0.090-0.093 ms), not deletable staging — it is the **7 318-bundle / 16.6% gap between the schedule and its own binding-port floor**, i.e. VLIW packing density, independently reaching `flash-attn-mha` v016's "substrate property, not a kernel lever". **Also load-bearing for every future bar in this family: the kernel is only 29.6% of the frontier (1.760 ms); the XLA projection chain is 62.0%** — the 0.116 ms bar is 6.6% of the kernel, and the dominant device-time block sits outside anything v017/v019/v020/v021 could touch. **THREE SELF-REPORTED DEFECTS, two caught by the verifier**: (1) the port decomposition silently folded `vmatpush` into staging, deviating from the page's OWN pre-registered bucket rule — 31.3% should read 21.9% under the registered classification (the fork was decided by the separate bundle-granularity census under the literal rule, so it is unaffected, but two instruments on one page disagreeing about its central fork is exactly what pre-registration exists to prevent); (2) **the LLO utilization matrix's MXU column is multi-cycle OCCUPANCY, not a per-bundle issue count** — 8.39% of its value sits on bundles carrying no MXU-tagged opcode at all, so aggregate floors survive but per-bundle causal attribution does not (a correction any family reading `-70-final_hlo-static-per-bundle-utilization.txt` should inherit); (3) every ms figure carries a **±20-30% band** from a bundles→time constant that `sparse-attention` v011/v012 measured at 1.702 GHz against this campaign's assumed 1.74. A registered opcode→unit least-squares instrument **FAILED** (residual max 4.24, negative coefficients) and is recorded as a failure. **Verifier lead 3 — same-mix rescheduling of the 0.29 ms packing slack, never attacked because both arms changed the instruction MIX not the SCHEDULE — is OPEN and becomes v022; the family stays live and is NOT at ceiling.**
- **mla-attention v019 cross-item prefetch / buffer depth (2026-07-27)** — **refuted at the MECHANISM level, and it closes the family's LAST untried class lever.** The question was never whether prefetch works but whether anything is exposed to prefetch: two instruments attacking from opposite directions bound the exposed per-program operand DMA at **0.02-0.083 ms against a 0.116 ms bar**. Pinning all four head-varying `index_map`s to head-group 0 (removing ~99% of per-step fetches) moves p50 by -0.0035 ms author / **+0.0158 verifier — the sign flips across sessions, so it is noise**; *adding* one extra per-head-group block (+268 MB, +28% operand traffic) costs +0.0223 author / +0.0233 verifier, **reproducing to 0.001 ms**. The buffering kernels (`pl.Buffered(buffer_count=3, use_lookahead=True)`, a {2,3,4} depth sweep, a single-operand lookahead variant) were **abandoned UNBUILT** under the stub's pre-registered arithmetic branch; the verifier was asked explicitly to sustain a reading in which deeper buffering could still pay and returned **rule-out SOUND**. **Why two instruments**: the constant-index-map probe ALONE could not have failed — under dead block-revisiting it re-fetches every step exactly as the frontier does and reads ~0 in both worlds — so the volume-INCREASE arm exists specifically because its bytes are real regardless of revisiting semantics (verifier-confirmed structurally: 8 custom-call operands vs 7, `memref<4x2048x256xbf16, vmem>` x 64 steps = 268.4 MB, a 9th `transform_N` index-map function). **Transferable finding**: the family's two standing wait instruments (v015's mem-stall <=0.121 ms, the LLO digest's ~0.42 ms), carried as competing estimates for four experiments, are BOTH loose upper bounds — they measure *potential* wait, and neither can distinguish DMA that would be slow if exposed from DMA the pipeline already hides; only add-or-remove-the-traffic probes can. **Process**: the volume instrument's stated consumption mechanism (an `x - x` fold) never executed — it was patched into a dead function and `infl_ref` was assigned but never read; the K6 verifier traced the live call path and caught it, and the measurement survives only because Pallas's BlockSpec fetch is architecturally unconditional. **All 11 class levers now tried-or-ruled-out** (stop condition 1 satisfied by set-diff for the first time); conditions 2-4 explicitly not claimed, family stays live.
- **mla-attention v020 graph-side fusion batching (2026-07-27)** — **refuted; all three arms regress, and with v017 it closes the graph side by agreement from opposite directions.** `fuse_kv_up` 0.9745×, `fuse_down` 0.9819×, both 0.9936× vs a 5.9034 ms frontier. v009 measured these same two levers at **−0.0001 ms** (neutral) three structural changes earlier; the conclusion transferred and **the magnitude inverted** — they now cost 0.109–0.154 ms, a clean confirmation of BRIEFS' "a lever refuted on one base is not refuted on another" in the direction that costs nothing to be wrong about. **The finding is the non-additivity and it is v017's mirror image**: additive prediction for both-on is 6.166 ms, measured 5.941 = **0.225 ms BETTER than additive**, where v017 measured **+0.147 ms WORSE** than additive for two *removals* on the same chain. Two experiments, opposite directions, one conclusion: **the XLA projection chain is a single scheduling unit at this op-point** — cost lives in how the whole chain is batched, not in any individual dot — so per-dot graph rewrites are closed as a direction. **The compile-only probe paid for itself twice before any arm was timed**: it showed `fuse_kv_up` moves the Mosaic payload sha (so it is a graph+kernel change, and the page's own `intervention_class: xla-rewrite` is half wrong — recorded, not relabelled), and that `fuse_down` makes XLA emit a **transposed** `bf16[2112,4,2048]` where every sibling conv emits `[4,2048,N]`, the probable mechanism for its regression (BRIEFS §6 layout rule). Self-reported: no independent verifier dispatch — author-side `kgate verify --mode screen` receipts only, justified by all-arms-regressing (a refute cannot be inflated by author optimism in that direction) plus the family's v015/v016 refute precedent, and stated so an auditor can disagree.
- **gqa-attention v010 opus5-process (2026-07-27)**: **inconclusive by the pre-registered rule, and the page refutes its OWN Mechanism paragraph — a premise test that cost 4 arms and re-based the family's whole ceiling.** The stub argued the v009 verifier's rank-1 lead (*"QK contraction `d=128` is half the v6e 256-lane MXU edge"*) was FALSE — that 128 sits exactly on the lane edge, so a zero-pad to 256 would cost ≥1.15×. It costs **0.9947× (author) / 1.0038× (verifier)**, inside the verifier's own ±0.43% null-arm floor. Not a silent no-op and not DCE: emitted matmul FLOP **+48.5%** (9.966→14.798 GFLOP) with the MXU issue census **identical at 7200/2304/2304/1152**, and the `qkpad=64` *truncation* — no zeros to eliminate — issues 7200 too. The mandatory positive control (PV contraction 512→640) moved **~21σ** on the same construct in the same body, so the null is interpretable. The verifier's **eight-point cost curve** shows the quantum is **`ceil(K/256)`**, not the stub's `ceil(K/128)`: a +64 pad costs the same as a +256 pad. **QK runs at 55.4% of nominal, PV at 98.5%.** So the v009 lead is CONFIRMED but is a **BOUND, not a lever** — an MXU contraction sums, so the idle half is structurally unfillable and `head_dim=128` is fixed by the model; the stub's own corollary survived its premise's death. **Envelope re-based 2.390 → 3.621 ms; the frontier is at 51.5% of achievable (not 34.0% of nominal), NOT at ceiling, with ~1.87× left that is provably OUTSIDE the matmuls.** **Author error caught by the verifier:** a PV/QK issue split of 72/28 extrapolated from ONE point was **inverted** — the truth is QK 4608 (64%) / PV 2592 (36%), from an over-determined 4-equation solve validated by a pre-registered out-of-sample census; had it stood, v011 would have targeted the wrong matmul. The verifier additionally **priced both remaining routed-class levers at ≈zero** (compact causal grid is already at the exact `(n+1)/2n` live-pair minimum, 9216/16384; grid folding targets SALU, at 0.4% static occupancy) and named the new rank-1: **bundle-packing against the MXU port, 35218 → 27917 bundles = 1.26×**, whose mechanism is the **capacity-1 EUP** carrying an 18 432-issue `exp` floor = 52% of the schedule. Three tooling gaps filed (get_llo_fit_summary + list_kernel_invocations both CRASH; `--expect-custom-calls` is vacuous on this family since XLA lowers the naive's softmax to a custom-call too; every campaign receipt fails its own input-regen check).
- **ragged-paged-attention v003 opus5-process (2026-07-27)**: **supported — new frontier 3.5837 ms / 4.7523x naive / 16.71% of v6e bf16 peak, 1.17291x co-measured over v002, and the experiment's second product is a CLASS-LEVER RULE-OUT.** The score tile is produced **transposed at source** (`scT = dot_general(Kg,Qg)` is `(nkey,M)` not `(M,nkey)`), so the online-softmax `max`/`sum` move from `axis=1` — the **1024-wide LANE axis, XLU-routed, 2 issue slots** — to `axis=0` (**SUBLANE, VALU, 4 slots**); the mask iota axes swap with the tile. Verifier n=150/leg, **both orderings agreeing to 8.68e-6 relative**, margin **17.3 sigma** over the pre-registered 1.10x bar. **THE TARGETED SIGNAL OVERSHOT BY 3x**: XLU-class raw instruction count **20208 -> 3504 = -82.66%** against a >=25% predicted floor (the verifier reproduced the frontier's 20208 EXACTLY on its own independent capture), with `matmul_count` held at 2048 proving reorientation rather than a work change. **THE FIRING AUDIT IS `PARTIAL` AND THE MISS IS THE LESSON**: predicted signal 2 was "VALU raw count **UP** <=15%" — the accepted **cost** side of the trade — and VALU went **DOWN 30.6%** (74177 -> 51472). **The stub's cost model was WRONG, not merely conservative: the transposed reduction does not TRADE XLU work for VALU work, it REMOVES work outright**, because the lane-reduction paid ~448 XLU rotate-reduce steps PLUS VALU combines that the sublane form also does not need. Second consecutive experiment in this family whose firing audit lands `PARTIAL` on a **metric artifact rather than a mechanism failure** — v002's was an occupancy-vs-raw-count inversion, v003's is a wrong-signed cost prediction. **THE OTHER HALF OF THE REGISTERED MECHANISM IS A CLEAN OP-POINT RULE-OUT, and this is the transferable part**: the MXU-edge-aligned PV is worth **nothing** here and is slightly **negative** — cand2 (PV output `N = D = 128`, **half** the device-queried `mxu_column_size=256`) BEATS cand3 (PV widened to `N = M = 512`) at **1.1729x vs 1.1692x** with PV width the ONLY difference, and the epilogue transpose the widening forces costs a further 0.0066 ms. **`gqa-attention` v008 got 1.3310x from exactly this reorientation and IT DOES NOT TRANSFER** — mechanism reason: here the PV contraction depth is `nkey=1024`, so the MXU is already well fed on the K axis and the column-dim underfill costs less than the relayout that fixes it. **Corollary: "output dim below the MXU column edge" is NOT a standalone defect — it is only a defect when the contraction is also shallow.** All three structural arms land within 0.3% of each other, so **the entire win is the reduction axis**. **NEW UNPREDICTED LLO FACT**: `matpush3` is **256 on the frontier and 128 on the candidate** while `matpush1`/`matpush2` hold at 256 on BOTH — *even though the PV output shape is identical between them* — so the transposed **QK** formation alone shifts MXU push-stage balance; and since `get_llo_fit_summary`'s `op_counts` regex still cannot see `matpush3`, **a tool-only reading would have missed the one MXU count that moved** (the known regex gap was NOT benign this time). **PROBE-DESIGN FINDING worth carrying to any paged/ragged causal kernel**: at `tps=64` << `kv_len=4096` every KV-block boundary sits inside the always-visible region and the causal transition lives wholly inside the **LAST of 4 page-blocks**, so a literal "block-boundary causal-leak" test is **STRUCTURALLY VACUOUS** — the verifier substituted page-granularity (`PS=16`) crossings inside the causal-active block and documented why. Correctness was gated well beyond parity because this experiment BOTH reassociates floats AND swaps mask axes: **18-position causal no-future-leak probe 0/18** with live positive controls (~40 movement on always-visible rows) and a reference calibration also at 0/18; GQA group isolation 0/3; per-sequence isolation 0/4; and the **full per-position error distribution over ~33.18M elements at ratio EXACTLY 1.0000 at p50/p99/p99.9**, flat across query position, no spike at the mask boundary. cand5's P=128 STEP-0 probe measured **37.09M vs the 32.00M scoped default — over by only 5.09M**, correcting BOTH a stale 58.09M on the family page (a v001-structure figure) and the v002 verifier's ~23.2M extrapolation, which makes lever 7 **compound** with the register-pressure lever. **NOT AT CEILING**: LLO still `STRUCTURAL — VPU-serialized` on both legs and **spills/bundle WORSENED 1.44 -> 1.62 under the win** (the same denominator-shrinks-faster-than-numerator artifact, now on a second metric in consecutive experiments) which **reinforces** the rank-1 register-pressure lead; multi-buffering ceiling upgraded **1.61x -> 1.83x** (`mem_stall_mxu_idle` 37.9% -> 45.3%); and the vector-only prologue/epilogue lead is **largely discharged as a side effect** (`vpu_only_mxu_idle` 15.8% -> 3.9%). Host dispatch is now 12.6-13.5% of wall at the SAME absolute ~0.45-0.49 ms as v002 — a fixed tax that does not shrink with the kernel. **Fourth independent confirmation** of the `kgate verify-receipt` input-regen schema gap, this time with a clean control: the `cmd:measure` wrong-math receipt validates FULLY clean, isolating the gap to the `verify` schema. Rank-1 v004 = register-pressure / live-set restructuring behind a **BLOCKING cand0 spill-classification diagnostic** (family-dependent lever: closed-by-diagnostic on `mla-attention` v015, refuted at 81%-of-bar on `gqa-attention` v009, supported +13.8% on `flash-attn-mha` v011; discriminator = whether the spilled value is the score tile itself).
- **sparse-attention v013 opus5-process (2026-07-27)**: **refuted — and it closes the family's largest unpriced term by explaining it exactly.** The lever queue's rank 1 since v007 ("issued matmuls achieve 61.5% of the v6e bf16 peak; needs a bare-MXU dnums-form sweep") is now priced. A BLOCKING pre-authoring probe censused the frontier's emitted `tpu.matmul` — 1280 ops, 343.597 GFLOP, matching the K1 table independently, in exactly two forms each carrying one 128-length dim against the device-queried 256 MXU edge (QK `M=1024,K=128,N=1024`; PV `M=128,K=1024,N=1024`) — then measured those *exact* forms on bare MXU inside a Pallas kernel on VMEM-resident operands (the frontier's own regime, unlike v007's XLA-level probe whose 232–424 TF/s could never bound a kernel already at 564.6). **Fed the kernel's 50/50 issued-FLOP split, the two measured rates predict its device-busy to 0.72% with no free parameter** (0.6129 vs a freshly captured 0.60851 ms) — the 61.5% gap is SHAPE, in its entirety, with no residual unattributed term. But it is UNREPAIRABLE at this op-point: the prize is ~all on QK (47.6% of peak vs PV's 84.8%), whose contraction depth **is** `D=128`; padding to 256 costs 2.00× FLOPs for a 1.92× rate (predicted +2.3% before authoring, measured +2.3% by the verifier's direct control leg), the penalty is **invariant to tile size** (44.85%@512 / 47.6%@1024 / 39.3%@2048 ⇒ depth quantization, not a pipeline-fill bubble), and no algebraic route packs two independent 128-deep reductions into one 256-deep one. Four in-kernel arms, all parity-PASS, best 0.9963×. **Two transferable findings.** (1) The mandatory restructure-alone control re-attributed the headline for the third time in this family: cand1's 0.7951× decomposes as 0.8137× operand-carrying cost × 0.9772× shape — without it the page would have recorded a ~20% shape penalty that does not exist. (2) **Emitted matmul COUNT is not a cost driver**: cand2/cand3 cut it 20→14 (−30%) at identical arithmetic and masking for ≤0.7% of wall, which RETIRES v011's "must not increase the emitted count" admission criterion that had been blocking the rank-1 prize (the above-diagonal fifth) — the right test is the *rate* the resulting shapes earn, and this page's tile curve is the instrument. Split-loop (≤11.2%, never implemented) promoted to rank 1 for v014. **NOT at ceiling** — verifier-confirmed. Also reported two kgate gaps with provenance rather than edited: `verify`-cmd receipts are not self-validating (`input regeneration FAILED: receipt lacks baseline path or input hashes` on *every* such receipt in the campaign, including v009's frontier — `measure` receipts validate fully), and `count_custom_calls`' regex over-counts a single `ConcatBitcast` as 3–4 ops.
- **ragged-paged-attention v004 opus5-process (2026-07-27)**: **refuted — the live-set-restructuring lever CLOSED BY MEASUREMENT BEFORE A LINE OF KERNEL CODE, for the cost of one compile.** The K3 stub pre-registered a **BLOCKING cand0 diagnostic** with an explicit ABORT rule; the rule fired. Four counterfactual schedules of the v003 frontier: `pre-ra` 19011 / `no-ra-deps` 19528 / `no-spills-fills` 21972 / **`post-ra` SHIPPED 22830** bundles ⇒ **attackable = post-ra − no-spills-fills = 858 = 3.758%** of shipped, below the pre-registered 5.0% threshold. Cap under **perfect** execution = **1.0391x device-only = 78.1% of the 1.05x bar**, tightened by the verifier to a wall-clock-correct **1.0340x = 68.0% of bar** by holding the measured 12.39% host-dispatch floor fixed. **NAMING THE EXACT PAIR WAS LOAD-BEARING AND THIS PAGE IS THE FIRST DOWNSTREAM CONSUMER OF v009's PROCESS FIX**: `post-ra − pre-ra` = 3819 = **16.73%** would have over-counted **4.45x**, sailed past the threshold, and spent a five-candidate authoring cycle on a closed lever. The unattackable remainder (`no-spills-fills − pre-ra` = 2961 = 12.97%) is register-**assignment** pressure present with **zero** spill instructions, which no source change removes. **THE DISCRIMINATOR ANSWERS YES**: a single-pass census mapping every spilled vreg to its producing opcode gives **`vsel` 31.0% (the CAUSAL MASK APPLICATION) + `vpop.eup` 27.2% (the EXP RESULT) = 58.2% of spills ARE THE SCORE TILE ITSELF**, at `(1024,512)` fp32 = 2 MiB against a 64-vreg file (measured peak pressure **1554 vregs = 24.3x the file**). **THIRD FAMILY TO PRICE THIS SAME LEVER THE SAME WAY AND GET THE SAME ANSWER**: `mla-attention` v015 closed it at 74% of bar, `gqa-attention` v009 at 81%, this family at 68% — while `flash-attn-mha` v011 **supported** it (+13.8%) when the spilled value was *not* the tile. **WHY 36992 SPILL+FILL EVENTS COST ONLY 858 BUNDLES** (the paradox a raw-count reading would have fumbled): 69.5% of bundles carry ≥1 spill/fill event but **99.8% of those co-issue other useful work** — only **28 bundles (0.12% of the program)** exist *solely* for spill traffic; the traffic packs into spare VLIW slot capacity instead of extending the schedule. Spilling displaces almost nothing. The verifier **reproduced every schedule count BIT-FOR-BIT (0.00% divergence** from a fresh dump at a different input seed — stronger than the 0.09% its own gqa v009 counterpart managed), proved the `vsel` attribution by **predicate-producer tracing** rather than assertion (of 5121 `vsel`, exactly 512 share one `vcmp.eq.s32` epilogue-`valid` predicate — an exact match to `(512,128)/1024 × 8 groups` — and 4609 carry per-element mask predicates; **100% of the mask-path results spill, 0% of the epilogue-path do**), proved `vpop.eup` is the softmax `exp` via the `vpow2.f32`→`vpop.eup` pairing, and validated the abort rule's own **soundness precondition** (bundles ≈ cycles ⇐ issue-bound) three independent ways including quoting the tool's source to show its `mem` classification is VMEM↔register issue slots, **not** hidden DMA wait. It also found a **real bug in this page's first-pass census** — the `vst_source=` regex matched 16441 events but only **14881** are preceded by a genuine `_spill` allocation bracket (the other 1560 are ordinary `vst` writes to plain VMEM) — and **every correction moved the refute in the CONSERVATIVE direction** (score-tile share 52.7% → 58.2%; cap 78.1% → 68.0% of bar). **TWO FINDINGS BEYOND THE REFUTE.** (1) **`get_llo_fit_summary`'s rank-1 `STRUCTURAL — restructure the live-register set` recommendation was WRONG for this kernel, and the tool cannot know it** — it fires off a static spill-slot heuristic blind to the 0.12% solely-spill bundle share and to the tile being unresidentable by size. **A `STRUCTURAL` verdict is a HYPOTHESIS TO PRICE, not a lever to author against**; filed to the master as a proposed `classes/attention.md` addition with the four-schedule pricing recipe. (2) **The next experiment gets its evidence handed to it**: `vsel` — the causal-mask application — is the single largest spill source at 31.0% with 100% of mask-path results spilling, while v003 established that **blocks 0-2 of 4 are fully unmasked** and the causal transition lives wholly inside the last page-block. Lever-queue rank 3 (**split-loop / unmasked-prefix**) therefore removes ~3/4 of the largest spill category *on top of* the VPU compute it was already predicted to save — now backed by instruction-level measurement rather than estimate. **P=128 compound CONFIRMED DEAD**: the only queued lever that could free v003's measured 5.09M gap was the one just closed, and lever 2 (multi-buffering) consumes *more* VMEM. Frontier unchanged at 3.5837 ms; **NOT at ceiling**; lever 2 now **unblocked** by this refute. Rank-1 v005 = split-loop / unmasked-prefix.
- **sparse-attention v014 opus5-process (2026-07-27)**: **refuted — the family's rank-1 lever is closed by ceiling diagnostic, and its sizing was wrong by 17× for eight experiments.** The lead every prior page named ("split-loop / unmasked-prefix, sized ≤11.2% by v006's M4, NEVER IMPLEMENTED") is corrected on both halves before a line of kernel code. **Premise:** the shipped body emits the causal mask on only **4 of 10** live chunks — verifier-confirmed at MLIR lines 351/429/489/531, with the residual 8 `cmpi` / 2 `select` confirmed to be *scalar* index arithmetic, not mask work — so v009's trace-level subtiling silently realized ~60% of the class-page lever and "never implemented" was false. **Sizing:** a 4-arm wrong-math ablation ladder (each arm deletes exactly one term; `tpu.matmul` held at 20 and issued FLOP at 343.597 GFLOP in every arm; independently re-censused by the verifier with a *different* script that classifies ops by vector-vs-scalar operand type — exact match, no dead-code cascade) prices the **entire** element-wise budget at **≲1% of wall** against a *measured* same-function noise band of [0.998×, 1.003×]: `exp` ≲0.8%, row-sum ~0.2%, and **the mask term is NEGATIVE** — deleting the 4 masks costs **1.4%**, reproduced in four processes at 4–5× outside noise.
